### 2026-06-17 to 2026-06-30 — archived 2026-07-04

_Newest-first, matching CLAUDE.md ordering. Archived during the 2026-07-04 lean pass (CLAUDE.md nearing 200KB again). Everything 2026-07-01 onward stays in CLAUDE.md._

- **2026-06-30 — MHT-CET 2025 April ingest, batch 3: the next five shifts (23 Apr S1 + 25/26 Apr S1/S2) → +748 PUBLIC, 1 flawed PRIVATE.** The "next set of CET PYQPs" after the 2026-06-28 batch (note: 23 April has only Shift I on disk → 5 shifts, not 6). Identical born-digital OMML `.docx` shape (all 3 subjects continuously numbered, separate `_AK.docx`) → **pure pipeline reuse**: code change = **5 new `SHIFTS` registry entries + one durable `cleanupArtifacts` hardening**. Per-shift flow unchanged (validate-as-you-go: 23-S1 checkpoint → then 25/26). MHT-CET PUBLIC 5,890 → **6,638** (Maths 2,231, Physics 2,221, Chemistry 2,186); total bank → **18,467**. State in [[mhtcet-april-2025-ingestion]].
  - **9 wrong AK keys fixed, every one contradicting the AK's OWN worked solution** (the derive-independently-then-cross-check model earning its keep again): **23-S1** Q150 (C→A, keyed plane fails the line's point; AK solution derives A); **25-S1** Q9 (C→D, n=3→ground state = 8V not 6V), Q16 (D→B, single-turn coil max torque BIA = L²IB/4π — the agent *under-called* this and kept the AK; I flipped it after deriving), Q148 (D→A, foot-of-perpendicular gives a²=1); **25-S2** Q61 (A→D, Ne partial pressure = He → n=1 → 20g), Q99 (B→D, carbylamine foul-smell test is positive for primary amines only → ethylamine); **26-S1** Q140 (A→B, limit = sum of 100 terms each →1 = 100); **26-S2** Q46 (C→D, true power V²R/Z² = 882W ≈ 900W), Q95 (C→B, ethyl Grignard + CO₂ → propanoic acid). Q107 (25-S1) kept **flawed PRIVATE** (plane "which does NOT lie on it" has 3 valid answers). 1 cross-shift exact dupe auto-skipped.
  - **The reverted-answer agent bug recurred (25-S2 Q61/Q99: reasoning right, `answer` left = AK letter) — caught by merge-shift's consistency check.** PLUS a NEW variant on **26-S2 Q95: the agent MIS-COPIED `akAnswer`** into its fragment (wrote B; the record's parsed AK letter was C), which would have MASKED a real flip as "agree" (ans==ak==B). **New mandatory pre-commit check banked: cross-check the agent's `akAnswer` against the record's parsed `akAnswer`** (`recs[n].akAnswer != q.akAnswer`); ran it across all 5 shifts — only Q95 mismatched, the earlier 4 were clean. So: always re-derive each claimed disagreement AND assert `answer != akAnswer` AND assert `agentAk == recordAk`.
  - **NEW failure mode — math-mask NUL leak fails the whole insert (26-S2 Q107).** A stem with malformed delimiters `\(AB \neq BA\[\){q:..}\]` (an inline-open immediately followed by a display-open) broke `normalizeMathFunctions`' math masking → leaked `\x00MATH0\x00` NUL placeholders → Postgres **"unsupported Unicode escape sequence"**, failing the ENTIRE 150-row upsert (the error names no row). Diagnose with a tsx one-off that applies the real `norm` and scans `charCodeAt(i)===0 || 0xD800–0xDFFF`. Fix: a clean balanced `stemOverride` (replaces the malformed stem before norm). Reconfirms the "author LaTeX stems via raw-string `.py`, never inline heredoc" rule — a non-raw Python `\text` also silently became a TAB this batch (caught + redone).
  - **NEW durable hardening — `cleanupArtifacts` glued-trig de-glue** ([scripts/mhtcet/lib.ts](scripts/mhtcet/lib.ts)): added `/\\(sinh|cosh|tanh|sin|cos|tan|cot|sec|csc)(?=[A-Za-z0-9])/g → "\\$1 "` (longest-first so `\sinh` isn't split; log/ln excluded to protect `\lnot`). The `\sinx`/`\cosx`/`\cos3x` glued-trig genre kept breaking KaTeX (26-S1 Q116/Q147 needed per-Q stemOverrides; with the rule, 26-S2 Q109/110/114/132 auto-fixed). Idempotent + safe for the 4 already-public shifts (they had no such cases). `\int`-glued was already handled.
  - **Figure genres reconfirmed + verified by viewing:** "which graph/circuit" 4-image stems arrive with SCRAMBLED extracted option labels → `optionFigures` + neutral `(A)–(D)` overrides, answer mapped by NATURAL image order and **verified by opening each image** (23-S1 Q20 phasor diagrams → B; 25-S1 Q47 diode reverse-bias → B, the −5V/+3V circuit; 26-S1 Q119 5-image logic circuit → derived q∧r, viewed all 4 options, S2–S3 series = C). **Agent figure-skip error: 26-S2 Q42** (4-curve photocurrent graph) — agent set `figureRefIndex:-1` as "text-answerable", but the options compare the curves → graph IS essential → set to 0 (attach). **Missing-figure recovery: 26-S1 Q12** — figure absent from the docx; reconstructed circuit + values from the AK solution into a self-contained stemOverride (answer 4.5=D robust). - **2026-06-27 — Cut Supabase egress (hit 120% of the 5 GB free cap): daily ISR on /notes+/guide + a calmer /browse sitemap.** The DB (~65 MB) + storage (~15 MB) are tiny, so it's a *transfer*, not data-size, problem. A 24h API-log sample was 100% `/notes` regen queries → **ISR regeneration of the ~500–650 indexed /notes+/guide paths is the dominant egress source**: cached pages cost Supabase **zero** (served from Vercel's edge), only regens hit the DB, and a 1h `revalidate` let crawlers re-trigger a fresh DB pull up to 24×/day per path. Fix: bumped `revalidate` `3600 → 86400` across 208 `/notes`+`/guide`+`/nda`+`/quiz` pages (content changes weekly at most → ~24× less regen), and dropped the `/browse` sitemap entry from `priority 1.0 / daily` to `0.8 / weekly` (it's the one uncached, heaviest surface). Deferred levers if that's not enough: trim `solution` (the biggest column) from the `/browse` *list* query; front question images through a CDN; Supabase Pro. Commit `7a7c47c`. Lesson → [[supabase-egress-levers]] (a sibling cost-lever to [[middleware-auth-scope]] — both: don't do expensive per-request work on public/cached/anon traffic).
- **2026-06-27 — Scoped the auth middleware to signed-in surfaces — killed ~40% of Vercel Active CPU (the whole Edge runtime line).** Vercel Fluid Active CPU hit **4h 2m / 4h (over quota)**. The Type/Runtime usage breakdowns showed the Edge runtime line (~1h 35m, 39.2%) ≈ the middleware line (~1h 27m, 36.1%) almost 1-for-1 — i.e. nearly all Edge CPU was the middleware. Root cause: [src/middleware.ts](src/middleware.ts) used a broad negative-lookahead catch-all matcher, so [updateSession](src/lib/supabase/middleware.ts) ran `supabase.auth.getUser()` — **a network round-trip to the Supabase Auth server, billed as Active CPU** — on **every public, anon request** (`/browse`, `/notes`, `/guide`, `/quiz`, …) that never uses the auth check. Fix: scoped the matcher to the four authenticated prefixes only (`/dashboard`, `/account`, `/upload`, `/uploads`), each `prefix` + `prefix/:path*`. **Not a security change** — every one of those pages already self-guards at the page level (`getUser`/`getSessionMember` + `redirect`); `updateSession`'s logic is untouched so the existing route-guard tests still hold. The only behavioural effect: a signed-in user's session cookie refreshes on those surfaces (+ server actions) rather than on every public page — fine for a public-first site (public pages use the cookie-less `createSupabaseAnonClient`, which never needed the refresh). TDD: 2 new matcher-scope tests in `tests/middleware.test.ts` guard against regressing to a broad catch-all; full pre-push gate green (commit `1d58461`, merge `2f123d3`). Projected **4h 2m → ~2h 30m**, back under quota. **Separate meter from the prior egress fix** — the 2026-06-27 ISR/egress commit cut the *Supabase* data-transfer bill; this cuts the *Vercel* compute bill (the middleware fix also lightens Supabase auth-request volume as a bonus). Lesson → [[middleware-auth-scope]].

- **2026-06-23 — LWS GAT Full Mock 3 ingested via `/lws-test-ingest` (150 q, 4 subjects) — Excel + bank-only, then reconciled against the teacher's official answer key.** Second multi-SUBJECT GAT paper (after APJ GAT Mock 5). 150-q NDA GAT mock — English (Q1–50), Geography (Q51–101), Physics (Q102–134), Chemistry (Q135–150); born-digital PDF, no printed key. `createPaper:false` ⇒ tagged Excel + bank ingest, no `/dashboard/papers` paper. **Semantic dedup vs the live NDA bank + heavy internal repetition** (Ring of Fire / Oceanic trenches / Mariana Trench each recur 2–3× within the paper itself): 101 new / 48 dup / 1 flawed (Geography was 40/51 dup — recycled standard GAT items). 101 new committed PUBLIC `question_kind='practice'`; dup + flawed stay out of the bank but ride the OMR Excel for Q-number parity. Built via **4 parallel per-subject agents** (transcribe + dedup + derive, each writing its own records fragment to keep the bank dumps out of context) + a serial merge.
  - **The durable addition — bidirectional official-key reconciliation.** The teacher supplied the official answer key AFTER ingest; diffed all 150 derived keys against it → **11 disagreements**, valuable in BOTH directions: **7 were my wrong derivations** (conceded — e.g. Q102 a lens cut ⊥ to the axis keeps its aperture → image intensity unchanged; Q58 the 105–145° band IS the P+S shadow zone, so statement 1 is true) and **2 were genuine errors in the OFFICIAL key** (Q4/Q5 sentence-rearrangement: the official option letters map to garbled, non-grammatical part-sequences while the coherent order is a *different* option) + 2 debatable recall items. User chose "use official key" → aligned all 11 to it (answer AND the solution rewritten so no row's solution contradicts its key), and flagged the 2 key errors for the teacher to fix the master. **Lesson:** the official key grades the OMR so align the bank + Excel to it, but surface the cases where IT is wrong — don't silently propagate a garbled key. Reconciling BEFORE `flip-public` meant the changed `new` rows were fixed via the cheap delete-source-rows + re-commit path, not per-row content_hash surgery. See [[lws-test-paper-ingest]].

- **2026-06-19 — LWS test-paper ingestion pipeline (`/lws-test-ingest` skill + `scripts/practice-paper/`) + NDA Matrices & Vector tests ingested.** A reusable flow for a teacher-authored LWS test PDF (an existing printed paper, e.g. "Matrices 6M QP", "Vectors_QP B") → THREE deliverables: the nda-tracker tagged-enrichment Excel (all questions, OMR Q-order), PRIVATE `question_kind='practice'` bank rows (dedup-gated), and a `/dashboard/papers` paper (the full printed test, all questions in Q-order, single section, created programmatically via service-role `createPaper`/`addQuestion` — the Atmosphere-test precedent). Generalized the per-paper one-offs (`build-vectors-b`/`build-matrices-test`/`commit-matrices-test`, now DELETED) into one `PAPERS`-registry-driven pipeline: `config.ts` (registry + `recToQuestionRow`/`recToParsedRow` adapters + `validateRecords`) + `build-tags.ts <slug>` + `commit-paper.ts <slug> --apply` + `flip-public.ts <slug> --apply`. Skill + how-to: `.claude/commands/lws-test-ingest.md` (gitignored, local, like the other project skills). Full state in [[lws-test-paper-ingest]].
  - **The load-bearing design — full test everywhere, deduped bank.** A paper references question *rows*, so ALL questions are committed (PRIVATE) + added to the paper + the Excel — **OMR Q-number parity is non-negotiable** (the student bubbles Q1..QN against the printed paper). But the *browsable* bank stays deduped: only `status:"new"` rows flip PUBLIC. `status:"dup"` (semantic duplicate of an existing bank question) + `status:"flawed"` (broken printed options) stay PRIVATE forever — they back the faithful paper but never enter the public corpus. This reconciles "add if not duplicate" (the public bank) with "create the paper as printed" (the full test); `flip-public.ts` is the gate (promotes `status:"new"` only).
  - **Two instances (both PRIVATE pending the PUBLIC flip — NOT in the 3,040 PUBLIC practice count above).** **Matrices** (`Matrices 6M QP 13-6-26.pdf`, 40 q, NO printed key → every answer DERIVED): 26 new + 12 semantic dups + 2 flawed (Q9 prints three identical options; Q25 the true `A⁻¹=CB` matches no printed option) vs the 383-q Matrices & Determinants bank; paper `bed3cfbd-…`. **Vectors** (`Vectors_QP B`, 120 q, VERIFIED key — no derivation): 102 new + 18 dups vs the 271-q Vectors bank, 0 flawed; paper `dc55cf9e-…`. Both papers + tagged Excels (`generated-papers/Tags_NDA_{Matrices_Test,LWS_Vector_Test_B}.xlsx`) are nda-tracker-OMR-ready now (independent of the flip). Pushed in two branch→merge→main commits (`6f44d42`, `629e063`); both passed the full pre-push gate.
  - **Dedup is semantic, via a subagent** (pull the chapter's existing PYQ+practice stems → too big for context → subagent returns DUP/MAYBE/NEW per question with the matching stem quoted; MAYBE = same template but different numbers/vectors/question-asked → kept NEW). No-key papers → derive every answer + flag low-confidence/flawed in a `reviewNote`; verified-key papers skip derivation. **Vision transcription + answer derivation stay the irreducible manual core** — the skill codifies only the mechanical wrapper + the verification checklist. Reconfirms [[practice-pdf-vision-ingestion]] (transcription) + [[cross-app-feature-execution]] (Excel-from-paper) + [[concurrent-selection-junction-table]] (the paper is a junction table).

- **2026-06-19 — RAG grounding + key-audit layer (migration 0040): `plain_text` + `solution_json` + `embeddings` + `match_chunks`; in-progress over NDA Maths.** First half of a RAG data-infrastructure layer that future AI products consume at runtime — first user: a grounded NDA doubt tutor on **nda-tracker** (the delivery app; the tutor chat is NOT built here — PYQ Vault builds the substrate). Pipeline + runbook in `scripts/grounding/` (README.md); pure helpers `scripts/grounding/lib.ts` (TDD `tests/grounding-extract.test.ts`, 31 cases). Full state in [[rag-grounding-layer]].
  - **Migration 0040** (advisor-clean): `pgvector` (installed in the `extensions` schema — so `match_chunks` qualifies the type as `extensions.vector` and the cosine op as `OPERATOR(extensions.<=>)` under `set search_path=''`); 4 additive nullable columns on `questions` — `plain_text` (LaTeX-stripped embed/ground input), `solution_json` (`{approach, steps[], final_answer, option_matched}`), `derived_model`, `derived_at`; a **generalized `public.embeddings` table** (nullable `question_id` FK, **unsized `vector`** — model-agnostic, no index at 2,160 rows; `model`/`source_content_hash`/`chunk_index`; RLS mirrors `0023`); and the **`match_chunks` RPC** (`security invoker`, JOIN-guarded `visibility='PUBLIC'` + `question_kind='pyq'` — the guard is on the JOIN, never the embeddings table, so it holds for service-role callers too). NO vector index (flat scan is sub-ms at this scale; IVFFlat/HNSW deferred to ~100k+ rows).
  - **The two derived fields split by mechanism.** `plain_text` is **deterministic** (`latexToPlainText` — keeps command NAMES like `\int`→`int`, strips only formatting; a first version that *deleted* `\int`/`\log`/`\sin` was caught + fixed before scaling). `solution_json` is the **agent** job — **blind** Sonnet agents structure the canonical solution WITHOUT seeing `is_correct`, so `option_matched` vs the verified key is a real audit. Scripts: `backfill-plain-text` · `export-batch` (omits `is_correct`) · `commit` (agreeing rows write; **disputes are HELD, never written** — the grounding layer must never contradict the verified key) · `apply-fix` (key flip + stem/option fix + `content_hash` recompute, collision-guarded). Agents WRITE their own `data/<batch>.solution.json` to keep output out of the orchestrator's context (the practice-ingestion pattern); a UUID guard skips a truncated id rather than crashing the batch.
  - **The finding that reframes the work: the blind re-derivation IS the "LLM-assisted secondary probe" that [[audit-probe-symmetry]] said could close the stealth-wrong-key gap — and it's ~90% precise.** Of 21 disputes flagged across the first ~365 NDA Maths rows, source verification against the local papers (`C:\tmp\PYQPs\NDA\NDA_Maths_PYQPs\`, 6 parallel render+rederive agents) confirmed **18 were genuine bank errors** (12 wrong keys + 6 corrupt extractions), 2 flawed-but-keep-official, 1 false alarm — an **~8% real error rate in "audit-closed" NDA Maths** (vs the 3.8% the prior probe-based sweeps found). **17 fixes applied** (`apply-fix.ts wave-src`); the `05c32038`/Q100 set deferred (doubly corrupt — both stems mis-extracted). So scaling the grounding is **two bundled deliverables**: the grounding layer AND a high-precision wrong-key audit; the audit is what *makes* the grounding trustworthy. User decision: run it as a **deliberate, batched audit** (ground in waves → source-verify each wave's disputes → `apply-fix` → repeat), not fire-and-forget. **State (2026-06-20, after wave 6): 560/2,160 grounded (~26%); ~40 source-verified bank corrections across waves; see [[rag-grounding-layer]] for the live frontier + resume point.**
  - **Not built yet (the retrieval half + the consumer):** generate embeddings (keyless model TBD — Supabase `gte-small` edge fn vs local `bge-small`, both 384-dim; Voyage a later eval-gated upgrade) over `plain_text`; the cross-app grounding/retrieval **API** on PYQ Vault (Bearer-secret, mirror `src/app/api/sync/mock/route.ts`) that nda-tracker's tutor calls; nda-tracker's chat UI. Tutor output validated via a faithful simulation (grounded vs ungrounded), not the live app.

- **2026-06-17 — Cross-app wrong-answer remediation: a `/go` redirect seam + "Learn this / Practice" links on nda-tracker's exam + quiz reviews + conceptSlug plumbed into the Tags export.** Answers "for every wrong question, give 3 practice questions — is it worth it?". The analysis reframed the ask (the literal "3 immediate clones" is massed practice + ignores error-type + balloons length); shipped the better shape — **teach-link as the universal spine + ONE bounded practice set, not 3-per-wrong**. Built for **exams** (the OMR/Evalbee papers — the real LWS assessment; an early build targeted the daily *quiz* review first, then the user clarified "exams") while keeping the quiz buttons.
  - **The seam (PYQ Vault, reusable): two GET redirects `/go/learn` + `/go/practice`** ([src/app/go/](src/app/go/)) that take whatever a delivery-app review row carries (notes slugs OR DB names) and resolve to the right `/notes` page / `/browse` filter. `subtopic` resolves **slug-first** (quizzes + tagged exam q) **then by DB name** (the exam path — exam questions carry chapter/subtopic NAMES, never slugs); every path **degrades gracefully** (unresolved → `/notes` index or generic `/browse`, never a dead end). Pure resolver [src/lib/notes/goLinks.ts](src/lib/notes/goLinks.ts) (`getSubtopicBySlug`/`getSubtopicByName`/`buildLearnPath`/`canonicalSubjectName` Maths→Mathematics/`corpusForSubject`); `buildBrowseUrl` gained `kind`. **Why a redirect, not nda-tracker building UUID URLs:** taxonomy resolution lives in the app that owns the taxonomy; nda-tracker stays dumb (passes strings it already has) and PYQ Vault can change `/browse` internals freely. TDD `tests/go-links.test.ts` + `tests/go-routes.test.ts` (DB-backed corpus selection verified live).
  - **Corpus by subject (the "practice vs daily-quiz atoms" call): Maths → the practice bank (fresh problems), every other subject → the PYQ bank.** Daily-quiz atoms are deliberately NOT used for exam remediation — they're Level-1 recall fragments (wrong rung for a missed full *problem*), and their one edge (concept-precision) is nullified because an exam miss only resolves to **subtopic** level anyway. "Learn this" → `/notes` is the universal teach-step (covers English + GK where no practice bank exists).
  - **nda-tracker side** (commit `44b443e`, wiring completed in `9b1b3ce`): `examLearnUrl`/`examPracticeUrl` in `src/lib/remediation.js` (prefer slugs, fall back to names) + the buttons rendered in the **shared `QuestionCard`** via a `showRemediation` prop. `parseTagsFile` (`src/lib/excel.js`) reads the new slug columns into the verbatim-stored `exams.questions` jsonb (no migration). **Under-wiring caught by a user screenshot:** the buttons were first added to only 2 of `QuestionCard`'s **6** render sites (`FocusedExamResult` + `ExamInsightsPanel`); the admin Exam-History view (`ExamHistoryTable`) + 3 audit panels (`ChapterAccordion`/`UnattemptedAudit`/`WrongAnswerAudit`) showed nothing until `showRemediation` was passed there too. **Lesson: a feature behind an opt-in prop on a SHARED component must be wired at EVERY call site — `grep` all `<Component` usages, don't assume "student + teacher = two components."**
  - **Step 2 — conceptSlug precision upgrade, near-zero cost.** [tagsSheet.ts](src/lib/export/tagsSheet.ts) `buildTagRows` now emits `SubtopicSlug` + `ConceptSlug` (the question's primary `question_concept_tags` row, via `getResourceTagsForQuestions`). **The coverage probe is the load-bearing fact: concept tags exist for ~100% of PYQ Maths/Physics/Chem/Bio/Geo and 0% for English/History/Polity/CA/Economics — exactly the `/notes` line** (English, the biggest GAT section, will never have them). So conceptSlug is a Maths-and-science *upgrade where it exists*, never the foundation — tagged exam q get a concept anchor + slug-precise resolution, untagged degrade to subtopic. It auto-improves as notes coverage grows; **no English/GK tagging campaign** (the teach-link is the right remediation there). No migration — the slugs ride into the schemaless jsonb verbatim.
  - **Gates:** PYQ Vault full pre-push gate PASS (commit `eb3e1ac`); nda-tracker 1540 tests + build. Not live until both deploy (buttons point at `pyqvault.com/go/*`); existing exams work immediately (name-based), future imports gain concept-precision automatically. **Deferred** (in SUGGESTIONS.md): error-type signal (slip vs concept-gap), a persistent "mistake notebook" + fixed-tracking, the teacher per-student drill-in (StudentQuizHistory rows → QuizReview). Cross-app mechanics in [[cross-app-feature-execution]].

### 2026-06-12 to 2026-06-16 — archived 2026-07-02

_Newest-first, matching CLAUDE.md ordering. Archived during the 2026-07-02 lean pass (CLAUDE.md had crossed 200KB). The `### 2026-06` header + 2026-06-17-onward entries stay in CLAUDE.md._

- **2026-06-16 — First non-Maths PRACTICE ingestion (NDA Geography) + practice pipeline extended for clean-text/no-key sources + a /dashboard/papers entry with its tagged-Excel export.** Ingested the LWS "NDA Atmosphere Test P1" PDF (50 atmosphere MCQs) into NDA Geography → **Climatology, Atmosphere and Weather** as `question_kind='practice'`, PRIVATE. Three firsts/extensions: **(1)** the `scripts/practice/` pipeline (built for the Maths book's vision-transcribed, answer-key-from-text PDFs) now also handles **born-digital clean-text PDFs with NO printed answer key** — `Topic.questionPages/answerKey/solutionPages` are now OPTIONAL, `buildRecords` takes `topic.subjectName` (was hardcoded "Mathematics"), `commit.ts` skips the answer-key parse when absent (answers come entirely from `overrides.json`), and `render.ts` guards the now-optional page fields. All additive — existing Maths topics untouched (typecheck clean). **(2)** No key → **all 50 answers DERIVED** (CDS-English style), committed PRIVATE with **6 `REVIEW`-flagged** lower-confidence items (Q2/Q11/Q15/Q17/Q34/Q36/Q50) for spot-check before PUBLIC; preview HTML at `scripts/practice/out/atmosphere-p1.preview.html`. **(3)** The per-exam `content_hash` dedup **skipped 2 of 50** (Q12 "gases 2nd in abundance", Q23 "not a greenhouse gas") because they already existed as PUBLIC NDA PYQs **misfiled under Chemistry (Industrial Chemistry) and Biology (Ecology)** — a genuine cross-subject GAT recurrence. Per the user, both were **re-filed to Geography → Climatology** (subject_id + chapter_id + subtopic_id updated; content_hash unchanged so no rehash) and their now-stale `question_concept_tags` (`ind-gases/applied-gas-reactions`, `eco-environment-biodiversity/eco-greenhouse-pollution`) **deleted** (else they'd be mistags — minor: each was a drill question in the Chemistry/Biology /notes concept, now removed). **(4)** Built a `/dashboard/papers` paper **"NDA Atmosphere Test P1"** (50 Qs, single Geography section, Q-order) via the `createPaper`/`addQuestion` admin helpers (service-role one-off, created_by=admin), then emitted **`generated-papers/Tags_NDA_Atmosphere_Test_P1.xlsx`** via the SAME `buildTagRows`/`tagRowsToAoa` the `/api/export?kind=tags` download uses — byte-identical to a UI download, for nda-tracker's offline-OMR loop. Including all 50 (48 practice + the 2 re-filed PYQs at positions 12/23) is required for Q-number parity with the Evalbee scan. Reusable lessons in [[practice-ingestion]] + [[practice-pdf-vision-ingestion]] (the clean-text/no-key/non-Maths path is the new variant).
- **2026-06-16 — "Trigonometric Simplification Toolkit" foundation concept added to all 4 calculus notes chapters (NDA + CET × Indefinite Integration + Differentiation).** A user-requested shared reference: grounded by scanning all **433 PUBLIC PYQs** across the 7 NDA-Maths calculus chapters (despace+lowercase the combined `text||solution`, `strpos`-count the canonical motifs, then read real solutions to confirm each simplification). **Finding worth keeping: in NDA *calculus* the recurring trig simplifications are the half-angle / power-reduction / perfect-square family — NOT the inverse-trig substitution table** (`2x/(1+x²)→sin2θ`, `3x−4x³→sin3θ`); those barely appear in calculus (1 and 0 hits) — they live in the Inverse Trigonometry chapter. The six recurring families (each in the concept): `1±cos x` half-angle (`2sin²/2cos²(x/2)`, `(1−cos)/(1+cos)=tan²(x/2)`); `1±cos2x` power-reduction; `√(1±sin2x)=|sin x±cos x|` (**modulus is load-bearing — sign by interval**); `sec x±tan x=tan(π/4±x/2)`; `a sin x+b cos x=√(a²+b²)sin(x+α)`; Weierstrass `t=tan(x/2)`. **Implemented as a duplicated `formula`-variant FOUNDATION concept (no `pyqExampleId` → 0 tags needed, and — confirmed — formula-variant foundations are fully EXEMPT from notes-lint's un-tagged-concept warn, unlike reference-variant foundations which soft-warn).** Globally-unique slugs `ii-`/`cetii-`/`diff-`/`cetdiff-trig-simplification-toolkit`; integration chapters got an ∫-flavoured worked example, differentiation chapters a d/dx one; `\log` (CET) / `\ln` (NDA) — moot here (pure trig, no log). Gate green (typecheck 0 · notes:lint 0 err · notes:latex OK); pre-push build prerenders the SSG notes pages so render is covered. Commit `49a7c68`. **Reusable pattern: a cross-chapter shared reference = the same no-PYQ formula-variant foundation concept duplicated into each chapter's foundation subtopic (cheap, tag-free, warn-free) — there is no single-source shared-concept mechanism in the notes model.** See [[notes-self-sufficient-template]].
- **2026-06-16 — MHT-CET Maths Differentiation /notes shipped (2nd MHT-CET notes chapter) — Phase-D reshape of 3 catch-all subtopics → 6 pedagogically-clean ones.** The bank's HARDEST CET Maths chapter (103 PYQ · ~45% HARD · 0 practice). Detailed analysis first surfaced that the 3 DB subtopics were **label-based catch-alls** scattering the same techniques across each other (logarithmic diff, implicit, differentiability, function-wrt-function all split across "Implicit/Log/Parametric/Special Forms" AND "Chain Rule/Product/Composite"); only "Inverse Trigonometric Differentiation" was coherent. **Pedagogy-first ⇒ Phase-D reshape** (greenfield: 0 concept tags, 0 sets, no MHT-CET guide, no code refs ⇒ downstream-safe — confirmed before touching the DB): 3 → 6 subtopics — foundations-chain (15, +differentiability) · logarithmic (19) · implicit-special (25) · inverse-functions (29, biggest) · parametric-higher (10) · derivative-wrt (5); teaching order set via `order_index` in the same transaction (so `notes:order` was already in sync). 32 concepts; **all textbook formula tables included** (Tables 1.1.1/1.1.2/1.2.1–1.2.4 + log-diff + parametric + nth-derivative as a no-PYQ formula-variant **foundation** so "all formulas" is honored without faking bank evidence). `cetdiff-` concept slugs (NDA Differentiation owns `diff-`; globally-unique per the mht-cet slug-collision pitfall); `\log` convention (not `\ln`, matching MHT-CET); **reused 3 existing viz slugs** (`diff-tangent-slope`/`diff-modulus-corner`/`inverse-reflection-line`) since viz slugs are a shared component registry → **no `VisualizationSlug` union or ConceptUnitCard changes, no new SVGs**. **Built via a 6-agent parallel batch + serial merge** (each agent authored ONE subtopic file off a fixed concept skeleton = slug + featured-PYQ + tag-group I pre-designed, so the output was mergeable and the 103 tags were predetermined; I wrote chapter.ts/index.ts/registry/wrappers + ran the tag INSERTs). 103 tags, **100% coverage, mistag-clean** (each subtopic_slug → exactly one DB subtopic). **Quiz-ready per Step 1b** (the daily-quiz discipline baked in at authoring): every recallable formula in `formula.latex`, self-contained shuffled-atom-safe self-checks, ≥12 self-contained traps/file — `quiz:coverage` came back **0 formula gaps, 75 traps** (no future harvest-rework). notes-lint #5 caught 3 worked-examples/self-checks re-using their featured PYQ's numbers (the logarithmic product-chain + sqrt-quotient concepts) → rewrote to genuinely different problems; 2 `whyItMatters` LaTeX leaks → Unicode/prose. Gate: typecheck · lint · notes:lint (0 err) · notes:latex (OK) · test · build → **prepush PASS**; all 7 routes live-verified HTTP 200. NOTE: the disputed-answer-key `a18fbd89` (sin⁻¹x²+cos⁻¹x²) was taught-but-not-featured, flagged for a future `/solution-cleanup` pass. Reconfirms [[notes-structure-pedagogy-first]] + [[reclassification-sql-pattern]] + [[notes-self-sufficient-template]] (parallel-batch orchestration) + [[quiz-formula-coverage-gap]] (Step 1b).
- **2026-06-15 — Paper builder: replaced the per-card `/browse?paper=` add-mode with a cart→paper bridge — fixes the param-drop banner bug by removing the mechanism, not patching it.** The step-2 per-card mode kept its state in the URL (`?paper=<id>`), but every filter/pagination/search navigation rebuilds the URL from `buildSearchParams(filters)` — which has no `paper` field — so the "Adding to …" banner + the whole add-mode vanished on the first filter (the documented param-drop class the `BackToNotes` pill sidesteps via sessionStorage). The fix wasn't a `qb_active_paper` cookie (the patch); it was the user's better reframe: **lean on the cart**, which is localStorage and already survives all navigation + reload. New model: browse + Add to the cart as normal → open the cart panel → **"Add to paper"** (an org-member-only button beside "Download paper") → pick an active draft paper (or "+ New paper") → **bulk-commit**. Each question auto-files by subject; idempotent (re-add reports "already there"). New: pure `planBulkAdd` (`sections.ts`, 6 TDD — dedup + section/position assignment) + `addQuestionsToPaper` batch (admin.ts, +1 integration test) + `listActivePapersAction`/`addCartToPaperAction` + `AddToPaperDialog` + a `CartPill` `isOrgMember` prop. **Removed:** `src/app/browse/ActivePaper.tsx` (provider/banner/hook), the QuestionCard active-paper branching, and the `browse/page.tsx` `?paper=` wiring; the editor's "Add from Browse" deep-link became a plain "Browse the bank" → `/browse`. The cart is now a hub: **Download** (solo Word) *or* **Add to a paper** (collaborative). Net: one fewer state mechanism, the bug gone by deletion, and the familiar cart (which survives filtering) is the collection buffer. Gate green. (Supersedes the step-2 entry below.) See [[collaborative-paper-builder]].
- **2026-06-15 — Collaborative paper builder, follow-ons: within-section reorder + assignee/attribution UI.** (1) **Reorder** — up/down move buttons per question row (user chose this over drag for a11y + touch/tablet + no new dep). Pure `positionForMove(orderedRows, qid, "up"|"down")` (`src/lib/papers/sections.ts`, 7 TDD) computes the fractional target via `positionBetween`; `reorderQuestion`/`reorderQuestionAction` UPDATE `paper_questions.position`; buttons disabled at section edges. (2) **Soft assignment + attribution** — the SectionManager dialog gained per-section assignee toggle chips (→ `setSectionAssignees` → `updateSectionTemplate`); assignees render as chips on each section progress bar; each question row shows "added by &lt;name&gt;". **Org-member names come from the service-role `listMembers(orgId)`** (reused from `src/lib/members/admin.ts`) because `org_members` read RLS is admin-only — a TEACHER's authed client can't read it, so the editor page resolves names server-side via service-role (scoped to the caller's org) and passes a `{id,label}[]` to `PaperEditor`. Gate green; user-verified the authed golden-path in-browser.
- **2026-06-15 — Collaborative paper builder, step 2: add-to-paper from `/browse`.** Wired the full-filter bank into the paper builder. `/browse?paper=<id>` (org member only) mounts an `ActivePaperProvider` that **repurposes the existing QuestionCard "Add" button to target the active paper** instead of the localStorage cart — questions auto-file into the section matching their subject (`subjectToSectionKey`); a top banner shows the live in-paper count + a "Back to paper" link. **Strictly additive + gated:** the provider value is null whenever there's no `?paper=` param (or the viewer is anon / the paper is finalized / the id is foreign), so normal + anon browse traffic is byte-identical — smoke-confirmed unauthed (`/browse`, `/browse?paper=<uuid>`, `/browse?paper=garbage` all 200). The editor links out to `/browse?paper=<id>` ("Add from Browse") so teachers get the full chapter/subtopic/year/principle filters the editor's embedded search panel doesn't expose. Touches: new `src/app/browse/ActivePaper.tsx` (provider + hook + banner) + small edits to `QuestionCard.tsx` (the Add toggle branches on the context) + `browse/page.tsx` (param → provider wrap) + an editor link. Full prepush gate green.
- **2026-06-15 — Collaborative paper builder shipped, step 1 (migration 0039): multiple teachers assemble ONE exam paper together, partitioned by section.** Answers "GAT paper is created by multiple teachers — how can multiple teachers contribute to a single paper?" Today a "paper" is the ephemeral per-browser localStorage cart (`qb:cart:v1`); this promotes it into a persistent, org-scoped, section-partitioned entity. Two new tables: `papers` (org-scoped; `draft`/`finalized`; editable `section_template` jsonb; `finalized_snapshot`) + `paper_questions` junction.
  - **Why a junction table, NOT a `question_ids[]` array on the paper row:** two teachers adding to the same section concurrently must not clobber each other. Each add is one independent INSERT row; `PRIMARY KEY (paper_id, question_id)` + `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` makes concurrent/duplicate adds idempotent (no read-modify-write lost-update window). Mirrors `quiz_atoms_map` — membership is a JOIN, not an array.
  - **Lifecycle mirrors the quizzes snapshot (0035):** `draft` is the live mutable junction table; **finalize** freezes the composition onto `papers.finalized_snapshot` (jsonb) so a later bank edit can't silently change a printed paper; **reopen** clears it. The export always feeds the paper's ordered ids into the existing `/api/export` `questionIds` path — **zero new export code**.
  - **The key insight — GAT papers are naturally partitioned by SUBJECT, and teachers are subject specialists.** So collaboration isn't N people editing one list (locking/CRDT) — it's N people each owning a SECTION; conflicts disappear by construction. The per-section progress view (English 48/50 · Physics 12/25 …) IS the coordination mechanism (falls out of the data model, not bolted on). The elegant tie-in: `subjectToSectionKey` derives a question's section from its subject, so adding auto-files (a "Physics" q → the Physics section).
  - **Collaboration model (user decisions, 2026-06-15):** (1) **org-wide access** — any ADMIN/TEACHER in the org opens + contributes to any of the org's papers (the org IS the boundary; no per-paper invite table). (2) **Soft section assignment** — anyone edits any section; assignment is a hint, never a lock. (3) **Multiple teachers per section** (native to the junction model). (4) **Sections fully editable** per paper (add/rename/delete/retarget); `section_template` seeded with a GAT default (English + GK subjects, ~150). (5) **Deleting a section is NON-destructive** — its questions keep their `section_key` and render under "Unassigned" until re-filed (any membership whose key isn't a current template key); no DB reassignment, no lost work.
  - **RLS:** org-scoped like PRIVATE questions — read = any org member; insert/update = org editors (`current_user_can_edit_questions`); **delete a paper = creator-or-admin, enforced in Postgres** (`created_by = auth.uid() OR current_user_is_admin()`). All FKs cascade from `organizations`, so the org-sweep `global-teardown` cleans test fixtures with **no teardown change** (advisor clean — no new lints).
  - **Access lives at `/dashboard/papers`** (list + `[id]` editor). Reachable via a **UserMenu link for org members** — teachers are redirected off the `/dashboard` home but still need Papers, so the entry point is the user menu (+ a dashboard-home card for admins). The editor: section progress bars, questions grouped by section (remove + move-to-section), an embedded search-and-add panel (reuses `queryQuestions`; auto-files by subject), editable sections, finalize/reopen, Download (reuses `/api/export`).
  - **Built ISOLATED — zero changes to `/browse`** (the only existing-code touches were the two additive nav entry points). TDD: 28 pure-helper unit tests (`papers-template`/`papers-sections`) + **9 RLS/data integration tests** (`papers-rls` — org isolation on read AND write, idempotent add, multi-teacher same-section add, finalize-freeze, creator-or-admin-only delete) — all green; full prepush gate green; new `ƒ` routes load (307 auth-redirect, not 500). The `/browse?paper=<id>` add-flow is step 2 (a fast-follow). Pure core in `src/lib/papers/` (`types`/`template`/`sections`/`admin`); UI under `src/app/dashboard/papers/`. See [[collaborative-paper-builder]].
- **2026-06-15 — Dedup is now PER-EXAM (migration 0038): `questions` unique key `(org_id, content_hash)` → `(org_id, exam_id, content_hash)`.** Surfaced during CDS ingestion: CDS-II 2024 Q44 silently vanished because it's an exact duplicate of an NDA English question (`NDA_GAT_2024_1` Q38 — CDS and NDA are both UPSC GAT English and **share questions**), and the org-wide `content_hash` index dropped the CDS copy. The user chose per-exam dedup so each exam's papers are complete and the cross-exam recurrence is visible under both exams (consistent with [[keep-duplicates-recurrence-signal]]). Changed: migration 0038 (0 conflicts pre-flight); `commitStaged` upsert `onConflict` → `org_id,exam_id,content_hash`; **`applyMockSync`'s two `content_hash` lookups scoped by `exam_id`** (else `.maybeSingle()` would throw once a hash exists under 2+ exams). Within-exam dedup (re-upload → 0 rows) + the MHT-CET mock-sync merge are unchanged (mocks are single-exam). The contract tests (`upload-commit-dedup`, `upload-flow`, `sync-mock-flow`) are all within-exam so stay green.
- **2026-06-15 → COMPLETE 2026-06-16 — NEW exam: CDS English ingestion (`scripts/cds/` pipeline) — ALL 19 papers committed PRIVATE (2,280 q); no answer keys → LLM-derived + confidence-flagged answers.** First new exam since JEE. Source: 19 scanned CDS English booklets in `C:\tmp\PYQPs\CDS\02. English\` (2017-I … 2026-I), **image PDFs with no text layer and NO official answer key**. So: vision-transcribe → **DERIVE each answer + flag confidence** (HIGH/MED/LOW) → commit PRIVATE for human spot-check before PUBLIC. New DB exam `CDS` (id `07700c16-…`) + subject English, taxonomy **cloned from NDA English** (8 ch / 29 sub — CDS English ≈ NDA English in style). Registered in `examContext.ts` (`ExamSlug` + registry → exam picker + `/notes/cds` hub auto-derive). State + resume in [[cds-english-ingestion]]; durable how-to in [[section-based-exam-ingestion]].
  - **The pipeline (`scripts/cds/`, mirrors `scripts/practice`/`scripts/jee`):** `config.ts` (paper registry + the **section-type catalog** — each CDS section type → NDA-English taxonomy + `underline`/`inlineStimulus`/`passage`/`perQuestionSubtopic` flags) · `lib.ts` (pure assembly + `validateRows` collision/coverage/delimiter checks + `normalizeQuestions` self-heal; **TDD `tests/cds-lib.test.ts`, 19 cases**) · `render`/`commit`/`preview` + README. Per paper: render → **section-map agent** (`data/<id>.sections.json`) → **transcribe BY SECTION** agents (`data/<id>.questions.json`) → **underline agent** (`data/<id>.underlines.json`) → `commit` dry-run → `preview` → `commit --apply`.
  - **The load-bearing lessons (from the 2026-1 trial → [[section-based-exam-ingestion]]):** (1) **section order/selection varies every year** (2026 opens word-pairs; 2017 ordering-of-words; 2023 word-classes; 2024 antonyms — 13–16 sections) → a per-paper **section-map pre-pass** is mandatory; (2) **Directions are load-bearing, not decoration** — synonyms vs antonyms look identical without them and FLIP the answer; transcribing by raw PAGE range split the antonym section across agents and silently flipped 5 answers (Q56-60, one unflagged HIGH) → **transcribe BY SECTION**; (3) **directions in `context`, one set per section** (mirrors NDA English: 887/900 directions-in-context, 895/900 in sets — renders once per section); (4) **fold the per-question stimulus (match-list code grid / S1-S2 pair) INTO the hash-bearing stem** — `content_hash` excludes `context`, so a generic stem + shared options collide + silently drop (6 dropped on the first trial commit); RC/cloze passages stay shared in the set context; (5) **underlines** via `\(\underline{\text{word}}\)`.
  - **Pipeline hardening as papers revealed shapes:** the section-type catalog grew to **~27 types** as papers exposed new shapes (`word-usage-count`/`word-usage-select`, `voice-transformation`, `word-meaning-statements`, `usage-statements`, `spelling-select`, `sentence-transformation`, `reported-speech`, …). `normalizeQuestions` self-heals three recurring agent quirks: options emitted as an OBJECT instead of array; difficulty synonyms/casing; **and a rearrangement-grid agent returning the ordering STRING (e.g. "QSPR") as `answer` instead of the option label** — mapped back to its label. The biggest per-paper gotcha: **"Ordering of Words" P/Q/R/S grids span both columns → render the FULL PAGE hi-DPI** (`fitz.Matrix(3.0+)`); per-column crops split the grid at the gutter and drop fragments (bit 2024-2 + 2022-2, both recovered full-width). **5-option spotting-errors** (4 parts + "No error") refit to the 4-option bank.
  - **Subagent content-filter gotcha:** a transcription/section-map subagent reproducing **verbatim passage prose** (the 2024-I RC section is Faulkner's Nobel speech with "doom/dying" imagery) can return **"Output blocked by content filtering policy"** (0 tokens). Workaround: have the agent do **structure-only** mapping (no prose, `passage:"TBD"`) and capture the passage separately (OCR/manual) so the trigger text is never generated by the model.
  - **State — COMPLETE.** All **19 papers (2017-I … 2026-I) committed PRIVATE, 2,280 q**, on `main` + pushed; every q has Directions-in-context + 4 options + exactly 1 correct; 253 sets. `Eng_CDS_2024_1.pdf` was Elementary Mathematics until the **user replaced it with the correct English booklet 2026-06-15**. **The ONLY open item before PUBLIC:** human spot-check the LLM-derived answers (no official keys), prioritising MED-flagged items + the oldest dense scans (**2017-1 worst, 60 MED**) + the part-rearrangement grids. Per-paper review HTMLs at `scripts/cds/out/<id>.preview.html`; per-paper detail in [[cds-english-ingestion]].
- **2026-06-14 — Cross-app tagged-sheet export: PYQ Vault emits nda-tracker's enrichment `.xlsx` directly (kills the hand-typed tags file) + passage context carried end-to-end.** The offline-exam loop uploads two files to nda-tracker — the Evalbee OMR *Results* (student responses) + a *Tags* enrichment file (per-question chapter/subtopic/options/answer/solution/difficulty). The Tags file was **hand-built**, leaking wrong-key / mistyped-option / off-by-one / forgotten-GAT-subject errors — root cause: re-typing data that already lives, verified + structured, in the bank. Fix (Phase A): PYQ Vault emits the Tags sheet from the built paper, so there's no human transcription left to get wrong. Shipped both apps, green (PYQ Vault prepush PASS; nda-tracker 1494 tests). See [[cross-app-feature-execution]].
  - **PYQ Vault** ([tagsSheet.ts](src/lib/export/tagsSheet.ts) TDD 19 cases + `kind:"tags"` on [/api/export](src/app/api/export/route.ts) → `.xlsx` via SheetJS (already a dep) + a third DownloadDialog button): `buildTagRows` numbers via the **same `groupBySet` loop the docx uses**, so Q-numbers match the printed paper AND the Evalbee scan **by construction** (the load-bearing alignment). Answer derived from the `isCorrect` option; subjects mapped to nda-tracker keys (`Mathematics→Maths`, `Current Affairs→Others`, GAT subjects pass through so `validateGatSubjects` is satisfied); a **distinct `Context` column** for passage sets.
  - **nda-tracker** (derived copy — **NO DB migration**): `parseTagsFile` reads a `Context`/`Passage` column → a `context` field that flows untouched into the `exams.questions` **jsonb** (Step4Confirm stores the tags array verbatim, no field whitelist — *the schemaless jsonb blob is exactly why context needed no DDL*; typed columns would have forced an `ADD COLUMN`). The passage renders above the stem on all four review surfaces (`QuestionCard`→ExamInsightsPanel + inline `QuizResults` + `quizTaking` take/review), so RC/Cloze are reviewable instead of meaningless stems. `NDA_FREQ_BY_SUBJECT.Maths` synced to PYQ Vault's 31 canonical chapter names (the validation source — `getValidChapters` reads it; the freq *chart* reads each user's persisted `ndaFreqBySubject`, so existing users are untouched; parent `pct` split to keep the total).
  - **The design spine — PYQ Vault is master (user-confirmed).** The Tags Excel is a lossy, schema-coupled bridge; the durable context work (jsonb field + render) lives on the side we keep and is Phase-B-reusable. So context went in as a **distinct field, never inlined into the stem** — zero throwaway modeling, nothing to migrate out later. The one disposable bit is ~5 lines of column-detection in `parseTagsFile`.
  - **Deferred — Phase B (the long-term win, user-confirmed): retire the Excel via a direct API push.** PYQ Vault pushes the built paper's tags over the existing cross-app Bearer-secret bridge (same pattern as the Quiz Factory `quiz-import`), nda-tracker stores it keyed to a paper id, and the upload wizard picks a pre-loaded paper from a dropdown — so only the Evalbee Results is uploaded by hand. Carries context/set-grouping/images losslessly (writes the same jsonb the same way → still no migration). The Evalbee Results upload always stays (it IS the OMR output). → ROADMAP.
- **2026-06-14 — New guide page: `/guide/nda-physics/ncert-map` — an NCERT↔NDA chapter cross-walk with a Class-12 weak-signal detector.** A new *kind* of guide subpage (the first source-syllabus cross-walk; not part of any template). Maps each of the 14 NDA Physics chapters to the NCERT Class 9–12 chapters it absorbs, each row deep-linking to `/browse` via `resolveTaxonomy` → `BrowseLink`. New files: [_data/ncert-map.ts](src/app/guide/nda-physics/_data/ncert-map.ts) (curated TS map — NOT a DB table, same curation-is-signal rule as guide principles), [ncert-map/page.tsx](src/app/guide/nda-physics/ncert-map/page.tsx), [tests/guide-nda-physics-ncert-map.test.ts](tests/guide-nda-physics-ncert-map.test.ts) (11 cases, written test-first). One `ROUTES` entry auto-wired the side-nav + sitemap; Traps' `PrevNextNav` chained forward.
  - **The weak-signal detector (the durable design — [[weak-signal-trend-detection]]).** The user's framing: NDA questions mapping to NCERT *Class 12* in *recent* papers is a leading indicator of syllabus drift — don't ignore the weak signal. The map embodies it: tracked Class-12 topics carry live-bank recency, and a pure `signalStatus()` helper classifies each **live** (≥1 q since `RECENT_FROM=2024`) / **watch** (tested before, now cold) / **dormant** (mapped, never seen). watch/dormant rows render muted with a "last seen" chip, so the page IS the detector — it surfaces a topic the moment it climbs. But the inverse discipline is baked into the copy: **we do NOT pre-write notes/practice for dormant topics** — chasing an absent signal is the same error as ignoring a real one, inverted. Map it, flag it, act when it goes live.
  - **The premise was checked against data before agreeing — and it didn't hold (yet).** Per-paper-normalized probes showed the recent Class-12 footprint is flat-to-*declining* (Modern Physics 1.9→1.3→0.8 q/paper; EMI vanished since 2017; Wave Optics/Communication never seen). So no Class-12 wave is present today. Two corrections fell out: (1) my initial 9/10/11 framing **undercounted** — a large slice of NDA already maps to Class 12 (Ray/Wave Optics, Current Electricity, EMI, AC, all of Modern Physics), so the map is 9–**12**; (2) the "missing rotational-motion chapter" is a **non-gap** — only ~3 incidental q bank-wide, accurately reflecting that NDA barely tests it (surfaced as the `UNMAPPED_NCERT` footer, not manufactured into a chapter). `signal` recency numbers are an authored snapshot (`SIGNAL_SNAPSHOT=2026-06-14`); refresh by re-running the per-topic probe as the bank grows.
  - **Post-ship fix (same day):** the page 500'd in production despite a green build — a `<NcertChip>` prop was named `ref` (React-reserved → `undefined` at render → "Refs cannot be used in Server Components"). The build missed it because the page is dynamic (`ƒ`, never prerendered) and the test was data-only. Renamed `ref`→`item`; curl-verified HTTP 200. Lesson folded into the "Recurring pitfalls" `next build` entry (build-green ≠ runtime-ok for `ƒ` pages; smoke-test dynamic routes).

Carved the other 20 into proper homes (subtopic_id only, no rehash): 12 river/dam/drainage → Rivers (15→27 q, now the chapter's biggest subtopic), 3 climate/monsoon → Soils/Climate (7→10 q), 5 location/extent/frontier → a **NEW "Location, Extent and Frontiers of India" subtopic** (the standard "where is India" topic, with no clean existing home; placed FIRST in teaching order). **Lesson:** a Phase-D split can both reclassify into existing subtopics AND create a new one for content that fits nowhere; and because the agent had already woven the relocated facts into the right notes concepts (the Rivers note already taught Tawi-not-Indus, Vitasta=Jhelum, etc.), 7 of the 12 river questions retagged onto existing concepts at near-zero content cost — only 1 new Rivers concept + the new Location note were needed. A new subtopic is purely additive (no rename), so nothing breaks; downstream sync = notes:order (order_index) + guide prose counts + the playbook `subtopics` array (so the new name resolves in the guide live-taxonomy test). Forests' %HARD rose 12→29% once the easy river/longitude q left, making it (not Mountains) the chapter's densest-HARD pool — guide prose synced across 5 files.
- **2026-06-13 — NDA Maths practice Batches 1–9: ALL 5 source folders ingested → 3,040 practice q across 29 chapters. Practice ingestion COMPLETE.** Resuming the 2026-06-12 pilot, the user asked to "complete NDA Maths practice, three chapters at a time, add to bank, update docs, repeat; some chapters may be split (e.g. Matrices & Determinants) — handle them." First pinned the **full Algebra source→chapter map** (section headers + first-Q markers): the source book's 18 sections collapse onto the existing NDA Maths chapters, several merging multiple sections (Sets+Relation, Permutation+Combination, Matrices+Determinants+System-of-Equations, Probability+Conditional+Baye's+Probability-Distribution). **Batch 1** = Sets & Relations (84) + Permutation & Combination (106) + Binomial Theorem (65) = 255 q. **Batch 2** = Matrices & Determinants (213) + Probability (133) + Binomial Distribution (40) = 386 q. Only **Mathematical Induction** is skipped (no NDA Maths chapter). Each chapter slotted into **existing** subtopics — zero taxonomy created.
  - **Orchestration: parallel transcription agents + orchestrator zoom-verify** ([[practice-pdf-vision-ingestion]]). One sub-agent per chapter (the 214-q Matrices & Determinants split across 2 agents writing temp files I merged), each given its Q-range + rendered PNGs + exact subtopic names + the parsed answer-key letters + the no-unicode-in-solutions rule. Agents transcribe + author solutions + **cross-check every derivation against the key and FLAG mismatches** (the key-cross-check catches option-order transcription errors for free). The orchestrator then **zoom-rendered every proposed override/exclude from the source PDF before shipping** — non-negotiable, because agents mis-read ~1–2 stems per ~100 q exactly where it's costly.
  - **The verify-before-override discipline caught 3 agent misreads this run** (would have shipped wrong answers): P&C Q304 (`=36` read as `=10`, key D was right), Probability Q800 (agent **scrambled the whole option block** A↔C, D↔B — the key D=1/5 was right; fixed the option order, not the key), and several "disputes" that were the agent's own mis-reads of the dense answer-key string. **~22 genuine corrections shipped** via `overrides.json` (wrong-keys + blank-keys across both batches) + **2 single-char stem-typo fixes** where the keyed option only becomes correct after the fix (M&D Q565 `(2,2): a→1`, Q585 `(1,3): cot²135°→cot135°`; same pattern as Quadratic Q212) + **6 genuinely-flawed questions excluded** (Sets Q57/Q82 blank-no-content, P&C Q292/Q315 + M&D Q571 answer-not-an-option). Legitimate multi-key "is/are correct" items kept as-is (M&D 650/656/687, Probability 734/807/819/820, Sets 60).
  - **Two agent output-format quirks to expect + auto-fix** (banked): an agent may write `options` as an **object** `{"A":…}` instead of the array `[{label,text}]`, and may use **difficulty synonyms** ("medium"→MODERATE). Both are mechanical normalizations before commit (a Python pass), not re-transcription. `commitStaged`'s content-hash dedup auto-skipped 1 exact within-section duplicate in Binomial Distribution (40 of 41).

Calculus is the most HARD/derivation-heavy folder so far (periodicity-LCM, L'Hôpital, determinant-limit, inverse-trig-derivative-ratio disputes) — the verify-before-override zoom pass confirmed every override against the source. New gotcha banked: a LaTeX `\\[Npt]` row-spacing inside a `cases` env trips `findLatexImbalance` as a false "unclosed `\[`" — strip the `[Npt]` (the `\\` row break renders fine).
  - **Batch 4 (finishes Trigonometry): +Inverse Trigonometry (104 q) +Height & Distance (16 q) → 16 chapters / 1,386 q.** 4 key overrides (all standard inverse-trig identities, book-solution-backed: Q1315→C, Q1357→C, Q1366→A, Q1367→B) + 2 single-char fixes (Q1394 stem restored to cos⁻¹x+cos⁻¹y; Q1403 option-c sign (√3−3)/2 → (3−√3)/2). No exclusions. The Trigonometry book is now fully ingested (all 5 chapters).
  - **Practice frontier: CLOSED.** All 5 source folders (Algebra · Trigonometry · Calculus · 2D Geometry · 3D Geometry) are ingested — 3,040 practice q across 29 NDA Maths chapters, all PUBLIC. Only Mathematical Induction (Algebra) was skipped (no NDA Maths chapter). The `.rar`/PageMaker `.pmd` files were never ingestable. A future supplementary source would follow the same pipeline (render per-column → vision-transcribe via parallel agents → orchestrator zoom-verify every override/exclude → commit PRIVATE → flip PUBLIC).
- **2026-06-12 — NEW corpus axis: practice questions (`question_kind`) + NDA Maths practice ingestion pipeline + /browse PYQ/Practice/All toggle.** The bank gained a second question corpus: **practice** questions (from the "Mathematics for N.D.A and N.A" practice book), kept separate from the PYQ corpus by a new **`question_kind` enum** (`pyq`|`practice`, default `pyq`, migration 0036). **5 NDA Maths topics live PUBLIC (335 q): Sequence & Series 84 + Complex Numbers 82 + Quadratic Equations 62 + Logarithms 26 + Statistics 81** — each mapped onto an **existing** NDA Maths chapter + existing subtopics (standing rule: never create a new chapter for practice; skip a practice section with no NDA Maths home). State + runbook in [[practice-ingestion]] + `scripts/practice/README.md`; reusable ingestion technique in [[practice-pdf-vision-ingestion]].
  - **Why a new enum, not `pyq_year IS NULL`:** the product is PYQ-first ("PYQ Vault") and every PYQ surface (guides/notes/quiz/paper-builder/counts) assumed the corpus was past-year. `pyq_year IS NULL` is fragile (conflates "practice" with "PYQ of unknown year"). An explicit axis lets practice coexist without polluting PYQ counts. User decisions: separation = new enum; extraction = vision-for-everything; pilot = Sequence & Series within Algebra; include solutions; difficulty = vision-estimated; multi-key = stage PRIVATE + flag.
  - **Ingestion pipeline (`scripts/practice/`, mirrors `scripts/jee/`):** the source PDFs are born-digital but **lossy in the text layer** (set/relational operators drop to blank, superscripts collapse, two-column layout), so transcription is **vision-driven** — `render.ts` rasterises pages **per-column at high DPI**, a human/Claude transcribes the PNGs → `data/<topic>.{questions,solutions}.json`, `commit.ts` merges with the **text-layer-parsed answer key** + solutions → `commitStaged` (PRIVATE) → post-commit UPDATE stamps `question_kind='practice'`. The `.rar` files are legacy PageMaker `.pmd` source — not ingestable. Pure helpers (`parseAnswerKey`/`buildRecords`/`findLatexImbalance`) TDD'd (18 tests).
  - **The verification discipline (what made the pilot correct — [[practice-pdf-vision-ingestion]]):** every question cross-checked three ways (image + answer-key file + worked solution + own re-derivation). **Both the book's answer key AND its printed solution can be wrong together** (Q486 keyed+solved "None" but sinA,B,C are exactly in A.P. → option C) — re-derivation is the arbiter, not either source. **MCQ option ORDER is load-bearing** (the key is by-letter, so a swapped option text silently mis-keys — caught on 414/444/445/450 only by 5× zoom-crops). 6 source answer-key errors/blanks corrected via committed `overrides.json` (with reasons; the commit prints them, the preview badges them). `preview.ts` emits a KaTeX-rendered review HTML.
  - **/browse toggle + PYQ-only guards:** added `kind` to `Filters` (default `pyq`, omitted from the URL when `pyq` so existing links are byte-identical), threaded into `queryQuestions` + the facet RPCs (migration 0037 `p_kind`, dropped+recreated to avoid a Postgres overload); a "Question type" segmented control in `FilterBar`. **The downstream-safety design:** tag-gated surfaces (guide principles, notes drills, worked examples, quiz harvest) are PYQ-only *by construction* (practice is never tagged); only the four **raw count-by-taxonomy** queries (`examHomeStats` + the 3 notes landings) needed an explicit `question_kind='pyq'` guard — added so practice can flip PUBLIC later without inflating PYQ-first surfaces. Verified live: Sequence & Series facet counts pyq=89 / practice=84 / all=173.
  - **The `content_hash` re-commit hazard (banked):** `content_hash = sha256(stem + sorted options + answer)`, so fixing a stem/option/answer in `data/` after committing changes the hash → re-running `commit` INSERTS a new row + orphans the old one. Fix: delete the affected rows first, then re-commit (others dedup-skip). Editing only `solution` text is safe (not in the hash). Same hazard family as JEE's resync.
  - **Batch ingestion (3 chapters at a time): +Sets & Relations (84 q) +Permutation & Combination (106 q) +Binomial Theorem (65 q) → 8 topics / 590 q.** Each merges its source sub-sections into one existing NDA Maths chapter (Sets Q1–35 + Relation Q36–86; Permutation Q233–282 + Combination Q283–340; all subtopics already existed — no taxonomy created). Transcription was distributed across **3 parallel sub-agents** (one per chapter) authoring questions + solutions + a key-vs-derivation dispute report; the orchestrator zoom-verified every dispute against the source PDF before committing. **The verify-before-override discipline paid for itself:** of the agents' proposed key changes, one (P&C Q304) was a *misderivation* (the agent read the stem's `=36` as `=10`; C(n,2)=36 ⇒ n=9, so the original key D was right) — caught only by re-rendering the stem. 13 genuine corrections shipped via `overrides.json` (Sets Q50→D, Q65→C; P&C Q270→D/Q296→D/Q299→B/Q319→C wrong-keys + Q255/276/289/291/326 blank-keys; Binomial Q356→B/Q379→B), and 4 genuinely-flawed questions excluded (Sets Q57/Q82 blank-no-content; P&C Q292/Q315 correct-answer-not-an-option). Sets Q60 is a legitimate multi-key (B,C) "is/are true" item.
  - **Scale-up (earlier same session): +Complex Numbers (82 q) +Quadratic Equations (62 q) → 5 topics / 335 q.** Both slot into existing NDA Maths chapters + subtopics (the Quadratic Equations chapter already held 63 PYQs in the same 3 subtopics — no taxonomy created). Recurring per-topic findings worth budgeting for: a 3rd-party practice book ships **genuinely-flawed MCQs** whose correct answer isn't among the printed options — **exclude those** (don't transcribe them; they show as intentional coverage gaps) rather than ship a wrong key (Quadratic Q202: book solution derives `m=(a−b)/(a+b)`, option c is its reciprocal, key file's `d` also wrong; Q213: blank key + impossible condition). And **single-char source typos** in the stem are common — fix only when the keyed option becomes self-consistent (Q212 `2bx`→`3bx` makes the keyed scaling answer correct; Q180/Q193 stems). Re-derivation against the worked solution + zoom-crops on every key/solution/derivation disagreement is what catches these.
- **2026-06-12 — Daily Quiz Waves 3-7: NDA Maths daily quiz COMPLETE — all 30 noted chapters across all themes (~120 quizzes built).** Operational detail + per-chapter status in [QUIZ_FACTORY.md](QUIZ_FACTORY.md); cadence in [[daily-quiz-pilot]]. Waves 5-7 + the binomial-distribution single-add finished the campaign: Wave 5 = binomial-theorem/properties-of-triangle/indefinite-integration/conics (12 quizzes); Wave 6 = inverse-trigonometry/trigonometric-equations/circles/logarithms (14); Wave 7 (finisher) = applications-of-integration/height-distance/binary-numbers (11). **Across all 7 waves every agent-predicted verify key matched on first run** (~50 verify files) — the parallel-author-with-key-prediction pattern is rock-solid. The per-wave detail below is from Waves 3-4; the structure is identical for 5-7. Wave 3 = limits-continuity, permutation-combination, application-of-derivatives, complex-numbers (16 quizzes / 225 q; all 4 needed formula+trap enrichment from scratch). Wave 4 = sets-relations, definite-integration, quadratic-equations, differential-equations (17 quizzes / 251 q; richer — all already had trap callouts, QE coverage fully clean). Each wave ran the standard cadence: harvest → `quiz:coverage` → **4 parallel agents** author computation distractors (re-derivation = the chapter's correctness check) + triaged formula/trap notes-enrichment + 3 verify files each → main agent re-harvests/syncs/verifies/assembles/pushes serially. **All 24 agent-predicted verify keys matched on first run** (incl. def-int's FTC/King's-property pieces that *re-indexed* when their `formula.latex` was enriched) — confirming the parallel-author-with-key-prediction pattern is reliable. Re-derivation caught + fixed 2 notes errors (CN cube-roots `1+ω+ω⁴`→`1+ω⁴+ω⁸`; diff-eq circles-on-x-axis order garbled answer).
  - **The durable lesson ([[quiz-formula-coverage-gap]], sharpened): "technique-heavy → park the formula/trap theme" can be TOO conservative.** Differential Equations' formula (8) + trap (11) first parked below the 12-floor as "technique-heavy." But several first-order-ODE items the triage dismissed as *methods* are genuine **recall** facts taught in the notes prose — exact differentials (`x dy+y dx=d(xy)`, `(x dy−y dx)/y²=d(x/y)`), the **linear-in-x** integrating factor, and the **Bernoulli** substitution `v=y^{1-n}`. Exposing those 5 formulas + 2 traps cleared both to 13 and shipped a formula + trap quiz. **Trigger rule:** when a formula/trap theme PARKS at 8–11 (just under 12), re-examine the triage before accepting the park — a *named identity/substitution taught in prose* IS recall even if it reads like "a method"; only genuinely formula-less procedures (substitute-the-point, log-differentiate, collinearity test) stay skipped. The "when in doubt SKIP / don't pad" rule still holds for true techniques; the refinement is that exact-differential/Bernoulli/linear-in-x style *named results* are not techniques.
  - **Thin-theme park is still correct where the content genuinely isn't there** — sub-12 themes that can't be honestly topped up (QE computation 7 q — QE is formula/trap-heavy with few practiceSet; Sets fact 8; Def-Int formula-3 leftover; binary-numbers formula 8; conics computation 3) stay parked + carry forward to a future cross-chapter "Formulas/Traps of the day" assembler (the documented deferred mechanism). Don't pad those.
  - **The recurring Wave 5-7 lesson ([[quiz-formula-coverage-gap]], sharpened): the harvest's "MCQ-clean" count CONFLATES formula-piece atoms with computation, so computation is systematically over-counted — most chapters actually had near-zero authored `practiceSet`.** Indefinite Integration had 0 practiceSet, Inverse Trig / Trig Equations / Properties / Binomial Theorem ~2 each, Circles 7 — yet the harvest reported 9-27 "MCQ-clean." So when a computation theme looks like it should clear 12 but the assembler says it parks, **count the ACTUAL `sourceKind:"practiceSet"`+`"selfCheck"` atoms** (not the harvest's MCQ-clean figure), and top up by authoring genuine practice problems into the notes concepts that have a worked example but no practice — which also enriches the student notes (real practice + "common mistakes" sections). This + the trap-callout top-ups were the bulk of the Wave 5-7 work. Genuinely computation-heavy chapters (Binary 39, Circles 17) need no top-up; genuinely formula/trap-heavy ones (Conics) leave computation parked.
- **2026-06-12 — NDA Biology /notes: 6 chapters shipped in one parallel batch (Cell Biology · Microbiology & Disease · Biodiversity & Classification · Plant Biology · Reproduction · Ecology & Environment) — completing NDA Biology to 7 of 9 chapters.** Per-chapter one-liners in [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md). **130 q · 81 concepts · 15 static SVG diagrams**, all reorder-only + 100% concept-tag coverage (130/130). Built via the now-standard **6-agent parallel batch + serial merge**: each agent authored only its own `_data/*` + diagram SVGs + 2 page wrappers under a unique slug prefix (`cell-`/`micro-`/`biodiv-`/`plant-`/`repro-`/`eco-`) and returned a structured handoff; the main agent serially merged the 3 shared files (`chapters.ts` 6 imports+entries, the `VisualizationSlug` union +15, `ConceptUnitCard` +15 imports/cases) + one combined 130-row tag INSERT.
  - **All reorder-only — NO Phase-D, NO guide sync.** The deciding check up front: the shipped `/guide/nda-biology` playbooks reference every DB subtopic name verbatim in `subtopics: [...]`, so any rename would silently break the playbook drill CTAs ([[shipped-chapter-rename-downstream-sync]]); the DB subtopics already formed coherent teaching units, so friendly display titles + `subtopicOrder` sufficed across all 6.
  - **Profiles match the guide strands:** the recall chapters (Cell Biology, Microbiology, Biodiversity — all ~0% HARD) lean `reference`-variant (disease↔pathogen↔type tables, kingdom/phylum tables, organelle facts); the Apply chapters (Plant Biology, Reproduction) mix formula-mechanism + reference (photosynthesis light/dark, double-fertilisation 2n+n→3n, tropisms); the Verify chapter (Ecology) mixes both (food-chain 10% law + symbiosis tables). Diagrams stayed **selective** per [[notes-diagram-decision-rule]] — 15 static SVGs only on genuinely spatial concepts (cell structure / membrane / osmosis / photosynthesis flow / flower structure / food-chain pyramid / five-kingdom tree), none on pure recall.
  - **Gates green:** typecheck + notes:lint (0 errors; 1 documented soft-warn — the `cell-organelle-map` reference foundation has no PYQ) + notes:latex OK + notes:order synced 25 subtopics (no name-join drift, confirming reorder-only intact) + a **mistag detector** (group tags by `subtopic_slug`, assert each maps to exactly ONE DB subtopic by name) clean + full prepush PASS. The combined tag INSERT's FK on `question_id` was the safety net for transcribed UUIDs (no FK violation = all 130 real).
  - **No wrong-key flips.** The agents re-derived every featured/drill key during authoring; unlike the NDA Maths/Physics "audit-closed" chapters (which kept surfacing stealth wrong-keys on notes-build re-derivation), the recall-heavy Biology bank held clean — consistent with [[audit-effort-by-question-type]] (fact-recall ≈ 0% wrong-key). One misfiled-but-correctly-keyed item flagged for awareness: `1f453564` (a human male-reproductive-anatomy question filed under the "Angiosperm Reproduction" DB subtopic) — taught honestly under a dedicated concept, no DB change (a future Phase-D reclassify could tidy it).
  - **Follow-up same day — the 2 thin chapters (Genetics & Evolution, Biochemistry, 4 q each) were then built foundation-led at the user's request, completing NDA Biology to ALL 9/9 — the first fully-noted NDA subject.** Initially deferred below the bank-coverage gate, but the user chose to build them as full foundation-led chapters (the topics — DNA/heredity/evolution, biomolecules/respiration/food-spoilage — are genuinely foundational + teachable, unlike Astronomy trivia). Authored **directly (not via agents)** since each is tiny (4 q, all 8 questions + solutions already in hand) and fact-sensitive: Genetics & Evolution = 2 subtopics · 7 concepts (3 formula foundations + 4 PYQ-anchored) · 1 SVG (DNA base-pairing ladder); Biochemistry = 3 subtopics · 6 concepts (2 foundations + 4 PYQ-anchored) · 1 SVG (4 protein-structure levels). **The foundation-exemption is what makes thin chapters viable** — no-PYQ foundations must be `formula`-variant (lint-exempt → no warn); reference concepts must stay bank-anchored (a reference foundation soft-warns). Both reorder-only, mistag-clean, 100% coverage (8/8). So the bank-coverage gate is a *default*, not a hard rule: a thin chapter is buildable when the topic is foundational enough that foundations carry the teaching and the few PYQs anchor where evidence exists.
