Foundations (M1–M3, 2026-05-08) remain above; only 2026-06 onwards stays in the active log._

### 2026-05-28 to 2026-05-30 — archived 2026-06-01

_Newest-first, matching CLAUDE.md ordering._

- **2026-05-30 (notes UX + guide drift) — Concept-level PYQ weightage table on every `/notes` chapter overview + closed the last guide drill-target taxonomy-guard gap.** Three threads in one session. **(1) Feature:** a collapsible "PYQ weightage by concept" table at the end of every chapter landing — the high-yield-triage map at concept grain (subtopic cards show per-subtopic counts but not which concept inside carries the weight, nor a %). Pure `buildConceptWeightTable` ([src/lib/notes/conceptWeight.ts](src/lib/notes/conceptWeight.ts), 10 TDD tests) groups concepts by subtopic, sorts high-yield-first, computes each concept's share of chapter PYQs; `ConceptWeightTable` is a collapsible server-component (parallel to `ChapterRevisionSheet`) with inline weight bars; `NotesChapterLanding` adds one RLS-scoped `question_concept_tags` query (PUBLIC-only, keyed by globally-unique subtopic slug). User-picked knobs: collapsible-at-end · % of chapter PYQs (dual-tags can push the column >100%, accepted) · foundations (0 tags) shown muted. Works for all 5 chapters via the shared renderer; no per-chapter authoring. **(2) Guide drift fix:** the same-day Sound notes Phase-D redesign (5→4 subtopics) left `/guide/nda-physics` Sound references pointing at the OLD subtopic names — the playbook's drill CTA, the strategy strand's `mustDrill`, and the CHAPTER_TABLE `focus` prose — so those drills resolved to an empty `/browse` filter. Fixed across playbooks.ts/strategy.ts/nda-physics.ts (counts refreshed vs live DB). The physics live-taxonomy test caught it. **(3) Taxonomy-guard gap closed:** discovered all 7 NDA playbook guides + Current Affairs themes ALREADY carry the `describe.skipIf(!HAS_ENV)` live-taxonomy resolution block; only NDA Maths (Template A — no `playbooks.ts`, resolves drills via principles.ts/compounds.ts/strategy.ts) had ZERO tests. Added `tests/guide-nda-maths-taxonomy.test.ts` resolving every drill target's chapter+subtopic against live DB. It immediately caught 3 more broken CTAs from the 2026-05-29 Probability merge ("Probability with Dice" → folded into "Probability via Counting") — repointed in principles.ts (×2) + strategy.ts. **Net: the 7 playbook guides + CA themes + Maths now all guard their subtopic drill targets against renames**; the `/browse` backlink-chip lookup (`getQuestionResources`) + the single-page Economics chapter CTA (1 chapter, stable) remain the untested cross-link surfaces (still the open ROADMAP "guide-side rename validation"). Also fixed a stale exam-context test (MHT-CET `notesPath` → `/notes/mht-cet-maths`). The recurring lesson — renaming/merging an already-shipped chapter silently rots its guide/notes TS cross-refs — is banked in [[shipped-chapter-rename-downstream-sync]]. 863 tests (104 files), prepush green.

- **2026-05-30 (notes) — First non-NDA `/notes` subject: MHT-CET Maths · Indefinite Integration (5th notes chapter).** Pedagogy-first ship following the full [[notes-structure-pedagogy-first]] workflow. **Bank shape**: 121 PYQs, the chapter's densest + hardest (~60% HARD), trig integrals ~55% of the chapter. **Phase D re-carve**: the bank's 4 method-named subtopics (one held 74% as a catch-all) → 6 teaching subtopics in a easiest-tool-first arc (fundamentals · substitution · trig-powers · trig-rational · rational-and-partial-fractions · integration-by-parts) via rename-4 + create-2 (no orphans, FK-safe order). The image-stub Q131 (`f543dddd`, a content-less "image-based integral" placeholder — no stored image) was rebuilt from a user screenshot into ∫(3x−2)/((x+1)(x−2)²) with 4 real options. **~24 concept units**, all formula-variant; **1 SVG** (`antiderivative-family`, the +C curve family). 121 PYQs tagged in `question_concept_tags` (100% coverage). **First-time infra**: new subject route `/notes/mht-cet-maths`, registry entry with `subjectName: "Maths"` (NOT "Mathematics"), MHT-CET `notesPath` wired in examContext, Footer link; sitemap auto-derived. **Two reusable lessons banked**: (1) **subtopic slugs are globally unique** — `question_concept_tags` keys on `subtopic_slug` alone with no chapter scoping, so reusing Sound's `foundations` slug cross-contaminated drills; renamed to `fundamentals` (notes-lint caught it, but only post-insert). See the Recurring-pitfalls entry + [[notes-self-sufficient-template]]. (2) **MHT-CET subject is "Maths"** in the DB — the registry/taxonomy lookup returns nothing if you use NDA's "Mathematics". **Textbook formula audit** (user supplied the MSBSHTE Ch.3 PDF): cross-checked the textbook's formula inventory against the notes, bank-validated each gap per [[bank-validate-notes-gaps]]. Added the px+q numerator-split (`linear-numerator-over-quadratic`) + √(x²±a²) log-forms (bank-backed: `74b316be`, `dd347815`); added ∫√(quadratic) standard results + tabular by-parts as **syllabus-reference** concepts authored WITHOUT a `pyqExampleId` (the bank has zero of those forms) and flagged "not a current bank pattern" in prose — these are lint-exempt as formula-variant foundations. Deliberately did NOT author the sec⁻¹ standard integral (0 bank evidence, no syllabus pull). Also fixed a same-day bug: `**bold**` was leaking literally into chapter landings because **`chapter.intro` + `chapter.title` are plain-text fields too** (GuideHero subtitle, landing cards, JSON-LD, `<meta>`) — stripped the `**` from the MHT-CET + Sound intros and extended `notes-latex-audit` to (a) scan ChapterNote fields and (b) flag Markdown `**bold**` / `- ` bullets, not just LaTeX.

- **2026-05-30 (screenshots, batch 2) — MHT-CET · Maths · 8 user-provided 2023+earlier screenshots resolve ALL remaining preserve-with-note rows.** Drains the 2023/2022/2021 backlog. 8 of 8 turned out to be bank-extraction errors (consistent with the 2024 batch's 3-of-4 rate; cumulative across both screenshot batches: **11 of 12 were bank-side, 1 paper-side**). Per-row:
  - **Q134 Diff · 2 May 2023 Shift 2 · `40cb6d56`** — bank option A stored as `√3 log(3)` but paper has `√3 log(√3)`; the inner √ was dropped. Derivation gives \(-(\sqrt{3}/2)\log 3 = -\sqrt{3}\log(\sqrt{3})\) = option C. **Option A text fix + flip A → C.**
  - **Q141 Indef Int · 2 May 2023 Shift 2 · `e836cd3a`** — bank stored stem as \(\int (\log x)^3/x\,dx\) but paper has \(\int \log(\sqrt{x})/(3x)\,dx\) (completely different integrand). With \(\log\sqrt{x} = \tfrac{1}{2}\log x\) → \((\log x)^2/12\) = key D. **Stem rewrite; key preserved.**
  - **Q149 P&C · 10 May 2023 Shift 1 · `5cd2bd76`** — bank options stored as fractions \(\{4/5, 4/15, 3/15, 2/15\}\) but paper has plain integers \(\{45, 415, 315, 215\}\) (OCR added slashes). Derivation y/x = 315 = key C. **All 4 option texts fixed; key preserved.**
  - **Q136 Vec · 11 May 2023 Shift 1 · `004f580b`** — carryover from the original 12-row handoff (yesterday's preserve-with-note). Bank options stored as integers \(\{3, 6, 5, 7\}\) sq.u but paper has radicals \(\{\sqrt{3}, \sqrt{6}, \sqrt{5}, \sqrt{7}\}\). Derivation area = √6 = key B. **All 4 option texts fixed; key preserved.**
  - **Q141 D&M · 11 May 2023 Shift 2 · `df815944`** — bank stem stored as `A^2 - 5A - 1` but paper has `(A^2 - 5A)^{-1}` (different operation entirely). Also all 4 option texts garbled (option B literally contained the string `REVIEW: answer key gives D`). Derivation: \(A^2 - 5A = \begin{pmatrix}1&1\\7&3\end{pmatrix}\); det=−4; inverse \(= \tfrac{1}{4}\begin{pmatrix}-3&1\\7&-1\end{pmatrix}\) = option B. **Stem rewrite + 4 option texts + flip D → B.** The most extensive single-question fix in MHT-CET so far.
  - **Q137 Trig-I · 15 May 2023 Shift 1 · `cf1fe413`** — bank stored coefficient as `3a` but paper has `√3 a` (radical dropped). With A=√3·a: tan(π/6) = 1/√3 = 2b/(√3 a) → b/a = 1/2 = key B. **Stem fix; key preserved.**
  - **Q121 Vec · 2022 Shift 1 · `c8dfd5a5`** — all 4 options misextracted: bank stored `{√36, 3/√2, 1/√6, 3√2}`, paper has `{3√6, √3/2, 1/6, √(3/2)}`. Derivation 3/√6 = √6/2 = √(3/2) = option D. **All 4 option texts fixed + flip B → D.** The "√(3/2)" → "3/√2" transcription is the inverse-fraction OCR error pattern.
  - **Q144 Complex · 2021 Shift 1 · `fc83f271`** — bank stored first factor as `(3+i)^3` but paper has `(\sqrt{3}+i)^3` (radical dropped — same pattern as Q137 above). With \(|\sqrt{3}+i| = 2\): \(|z| = 2^3 \cdot 25/100 = 2\) = option B. **Stem fix + flip C → B.**
  Net: 4 stem fixes + 5 option-text overhauls + 3 key flips (Q134 A→C, Q141 D&M D→B, Q121 Vec B→D, Q144 Complex C→B — that's 4 flips actually). 8 hash refreshes. Preserve-with-note count in subject: 9 → **0**. **MHT-CET Maths content-correctness audit is now fully closed at the probe-flagged + screenshot-verified surface.**
  **Cumulative subject totals (today's session)**: **53 rows touched** across 26 chapters in MHT-CET Maths (12 from /latex-cleanup handoff + 33 from REVIEW/hedge sweep + 4 from 2024 screenshot batch + 8 from 2023+earlier screenshot batch). Phase 5 probes (matches_option_disagrees, review_markers, hedge_phrases) all return 0 across 1,588 PUBLIC rows.
  **Pattern across both screenshot batches** (12 rows total, 11 bank-side + 1 paper-side):
  - Bank extraction errors split into clusters by genre: (1) **radical-dropping** — bank stored `√x` as `x` (Q134, Q137, Q144 Complex; also Q136 Vec where ALL options lost their √; and Q133 Limits 2024 where bracket structure was lost); (2) **fraction-parsing** — bank flattened `(a/b)·(c/d)^n` into `(ac·d^n)/(b·d^n)` or similar (Q131 Binom 2024; Q149 P&C; Q121 Vec where `√(3/2)` was misread as `3/√2`); (3) **operator-shape misparse** — bank stored `(A²-5A)⁻¹` as `A²-5A-1` (Q141 D&M); (4) **mid-expression parse error** — bank stored `(|x|+5)/(x²+1)` as `|x| + 5/(x²+1)` (Q114 SRF 2024).
  - Genuine paper-side issue (1 of 12): Q141 Vec 2024 — printed paper option B genuinely shows "30" but math gives 30√3. The official key marks B; preserved with note that the typesetter dropped √3.
  - **Implication for ingestion**: a single character-level OCR / extraction fix at the Excel-input step (preserving radicals, parentheses, exponents, fraction bars) would have prevented ~80% of MHT-CET Maths content-correctness defects. Worth folding into the upload-time validation pipeline.

- **2026-05-30 — Solution cleanup: MHT-CET · Maths (12-row handoff from /latex-cleanup; first MHT-CET content-correctness pass).** 12 questions touched (2 AGREE solution rewrites, 6 key flips, 3 stem-extraction-error fixes with explanatory prose, 1 preserved-with-note paper anomaly). **Key flips (6, all clean — derivation lands exactly on a listed option, no PDF needed)**:

  - Q141 Def Int 2022 May · `c6afb94d`: B=3 → D=2. ∫₀²[x]dx = 1; ∫₀²|x−1|dx = 1; sum = 2.
  - Q112 Lin Prog 2022 May · `4a995b10`: C=28 → A=40. Feasible vertices (1,0), (8,0), (12/5,14/5) with Z = 5, 40, 17.6; max at (8,0) = 40.
  - Q103 Math Logic 2022 May · `2bba9224`: C=(T,T,F) → A=(F,T,F). n²+n always even (p=T); n²−n = n(n−1) also always even, NOT odd (q=F); p∧q=F, p∨q=T, p→q=F.
  - Q124 P&C 2021 May · `cb8ad327`: A=60 → B=80. HAVANA 6!/3! = 120; VN-adjacent = 2·(5!/3!) = 40; not-adjacent = 80.
  - Q139 Straight Line 2023 May · `23d6cab1`: A=x+y=4 → B=x−y+4=0. Normal form at α=135°, p=2√2 gives x·cos135° + y·sin135° = 2√2 → −x+y=4. Key A has the correct distance but α=45°.

- **2026-05-30 (redesign) — NDA Physics Sound chapter — pedagogy-first redesign same day as ship; bank-first regression caught and reversed.** Earlier in the day I shipped Sound bank-first — accepted the 5 DB subtopics from the upload taxonomy without running step 0 of the Notes editorial workflow (the pedagogy-first probe per [[notes-structure-pedagogy-first]] that Probability followed two days earlier). The shipped chapter passed all gates but read incoherently top-to-bottom: subtopic 1 "Properties of Sound Waves" glued together three unrelated ideas (what sound is + perceptual triad + behaviour checklist), the ear-anatomy concept lived in subtopic 4 four pages away from the perception concept it explains, frequency bands were taught twice (in Wave Properties + in SONAR), and there was no narrative arc across subtopics. User flagged after a quick post-ship review. Redesign: 5 DB subtopics → 4 in a coherent *what → measure → behave → use* progression via a Phase D DO-block (renamed 3 subtopics, deleted 2, created 1, moved 10 questions between subtopics — all atomic, original UUIDs preserved for the 3 renamed subtopics so external links don't break). New structure: (1) Foundations — Sound, Perception, the Ear chain (11 q, 3 concepts including a new `human-ear-chain` reference extracted from old ear-and-instruments); (2) Wave Equation, Speed, Bands (13 q, 3 concepts including a merged `frequency-bands-and-scales` reference that absorbs the duplicate ultrasonic-band content); (3) Sound Behaviours — Reflection, Echo, Reverberation, Beats (5 q, 4 concepts); (4) Applications — SONAR, Transducers, Instruments (5 q, 3 concepts including a new `musical-instruments` reference split out from the old ear-and-instruments). All 34 existing `question_concept_tags` rows deleted + 34 new rows inserted with new (subtopic_slug, concept_slug) pairs. Same 13 concept-count total, same 7 formula + 6 reference split. Same 5 visualizations, reassigned to new concept homes. New routes shipped at `/notes/nda-physics/sound/{foundations,wave-equation-and-bands,sound-behaviours,applications}`; old routes 404 (chapter only existed for ~30 min before redesign — no external indexing). **Lesson — re-iterated in the [[notes-structure-pedagogy-first]] memory: step 0 is non-negotiable for every chapter regardless of bank size or "obvious" subtopic structure; the smaller the chapter, the bigger the temptation to skip step 0 and the bigger the relative cost of post-ship fix.** Same-day prevention: the memory's "Regression precedent — DO NOT REPEAT" section now names Sound explicitly so future chapter ships can't repeat the slip.

- **2026-05-30 (later) — /notes template extension: `ConceptUnit` discriminated union + reference variant; NDA Physics Sound chapter ships (1st reference-variant, 1st non-Maths /notes subject).** Reframed the recall-strand authoring problem. The existing template (Statistics/Vectors/Probability) had one ConceptUnit shape — every concept needs a `formula?` + a required `authoredExample`. That works for technique-driven chapters but strains for Recall-strand chapters where the bank tests memorisation of flat tables (frequency bands, SONAR, ear anatomy, sound-speed-in-medium). Forcing reference content into "intuition → definition → formula → worked example" produced padded ConceptUnits ("Q: A bat emits 40 kHz; is this ultrasonic? A: Yes since 40 > 20"). **Template extension** (Phases 1+2 of the ship): `ConceptUnit` became a **discriminated union** `ConceptUnitFormula | ConceptUnitReference` gated by `kind: "formula" | "reference"`. Formula variant carries `formula?` + required `authoredExample` (existing shape). Reference variant carries required `table: ReferenceTable` with 2–5 columns, ≥1 rows (cell-count parity enforced by notes-lint), optional per-row `noteAmber` (amber-tinted callout) + per-row `pyqExampleId` (renders a `[Q]` chip linking to /browse). Both share intuition/definition/visualizationSlug/selfCheckExample/practiceSet/pyqExampleId/traps slots — only the core teaching slot differs. New `ReferenceTableBlock` component (full + `compact` modes) renders bordered tables with RichText cells. `splitNoteIntoSlides` emits a new `reference-table` slide variant in place of `authored-example` for reference concepts. `deriveSummary` adds a `references: SummaryReference[]` array; both `SubtopicSummary` + `ChapterRevisionSheet` render a "Reference tables (N)" section with per-concept disclosures (the full table inline behind the disclosure — revision IS the table for recall concepts). `notes-lint` validates table shape + walks per-row `pyqExampleId` against the same PUBLIC + in-parent-subtopic gates; foundation-exemption (check 4 silently skips formula concepts with no `pyqExampleId`) is **formula-variant only** — reference concepts MUST have ≥1 tagged question since they're the bucket for the subtopic's flat-fact recall. `notes-latex-audit` adds `table.columns[]` to the plain-text-no-LaTeX checklist; cells, caption, and noteAmber are KaTeX-aware. **Discriminator decision**: explicit-required (`kind` is mandatory) over implicit-default, paid the one-time cost of a mechanical sweep adding `kind: "formula" as const,` to all 83 existing concepts across Stats/Vectors/Probability. Pays back in cleaner narrowing + a lint pathway to enforce. **Sound chapter ship** (Phases 3+4): 5 subtopics × 13 concept units (7 formula + 6 reference) under new `/notes/nda-physics` subject route. Pedagogical split: Sound's bank tests BOTH technique-driven concepts (v=fλ at `wave-equation-and-units`, beats at `beats-formation`, echo distance at `echo-and-minimum-distance`) AND named-fact recall (frequency bands at `frequency-bands-and-scales`, SONAR at `sonar-and-applications`, transducer chain at `sound-transducers`, ear anatomy at `ear-and-instruments`, properties checklist at `sound-properties-checklist`) — each subtopic mixes the two variants. 5 new visualizations (`compression-rarefaction-wave`, `beats-envelope`, `ear-anatomy`, `frequency-spectrum-strip`, `echo-geometry` — all static SVG, server components, bringing the diagram set to 44). 34 PUBLIC questions tagged in `question_concept_tags` (100% coverage). Tagging session unset 2 degenerate `set_id`s (two Statement-I/II questions grouped only by question format, no shared passage) before tagging — co-location rule preserved without dual-tagging. `nda-physics/page.tsx` is the new subject-index landing (CollectionPage with chapter cards, parallel to `nda-maths/page.tsx`). Footer swapped the per-chapter "Statistics Notes" link for two subject-level links (`NDA Maths Notes` + `NDA Physics Notes`); `/nda` exam home Teaching-notes grid now shows 4 cards (Statistics + Vectors + Probability + Sound) with a per-card `subject` field instead of hardcoded "NDA Mathematics". Sitemap auto-derives the 7 new routes from `NOTES_CHAPTERS` — no manual sitemap edit needed. 849/849 vitest green; typecheck + lint + build + notes:lint + notes:latex all clean throughout.

- **2026-05-30 — /notes interactive-diagram program: all-SVG, 3 batches, 39 diagrams.** Grew the per-concept visualization set from 9 → 39 across Statistics/Vectors/Probability and locked the rendering approach. **Renderer decision — all hand-rolled SVG; three.js/react-three-fiber evaluated and REJECTED.** Built a WebGL parallelepiped next to an SVG one to compare; SVG won on bundle size + mobile/low-end-phone performance + visual consistency with the flat set (the one genuine WebGL candidate — the scalar-triple-product solid — ships as a painter's-order SVG box instead). Static figures are server components (no client JS); interactive + rotatable ones are `"use client"`. Rotatable "3-D" uses an orthographic turntable projection that **follows the cursor** (`az -= dx`, `el += dy`; the naive `+dx`/`-dy` feels inverted). Banked gotcha: an SVG `fill` opacity modifier must be a standard step — a non-generated `/8` silently falls back to SVG-default black. Shipped in three commits: a polish pass on the original 9 (off-canvas clipping fixes on vector-add/cross + variance squares, unified indigo wrapper + `max-w-md`, `ceb2995`); a **High-tier batch of 13** (`87049ea`); a **Medium-tier batch of 17** (`ed39916`). Every diagram wires through the `VisualizationSlug` union + `renderVisualization` dispatcher + per-concept `visualizationSlug`. A full per-concept audit of all 83 concept units drove the High/Medium selection — ~43 concepts deliberately stay text-only (algebraic, no spatial setup to draw). Program status + remaining gaps live in [[visualizations-status]] memory. Per-chapter share: Statistics 12, Vectors 17 (carries the rotatable 3-D figures), Probability 10.

- **2026-05-29 — NDA Probability /notes chapter (3rd chapter) + shared-card credibility + bold/bullet rendering.** Four related ships in one session. **(1) Probability chapter, pedagogy-first.** Decided the structure from the teaching arc (*define → count → add → multiply → condition → bound*), NOT the bank's /browse-oriented subtopic split — the user's rule "bank is guide" (captured as step 0 of the Notes editorial workflow + [[notes-structure-pedagogy-first]]). To make the notes coherent, the bank was reshaped to match: a Phase-D merge folded the dice/coins/arrangements/balls subtopics into "Probability via Counting" (162 q preserved, NDA Maths 113 → 109 sub) so one "Classical Probability & Counting" page replaces four repetitive ones. Shipped 5 pages / 24 concept units, all bank-anchored to verified in-subtopic PYQs; 162 q tagged in `question_concept_tags` (100% coverage, set-siblings co-located, the mis-filed "sock" combinations Q cross-tagged to the Classical page). Phase 1 = the 4 backbone pages; Phase 2 = Bounds + 3 bespoke visualizations. **(2) Set-bound PYQ context now renders inline.** `loadWorkedExamples` + `WorkedExample` gained `context`; `WorkedExampleCard` renders the passage above the question (stripping the "for the next N items" count phrase via `stripPassageCountPhrase`) so featured PYQs + mastery-checkpoint questions that are passage-set siblings are solvable. Was a latent gap on `/guide` too (shared card). **(3) PYQ provenance citation.** `loadWorkedExamples` composes a `provenance` string via the existing `formatProvenance`; `WorkedExampleCard` shows `[Q# · month · year]` below the question — same convention as `/browse`, for credibility. **(4) `**bold**` + bullet definitions.** The notes renderer dropped Markdown bold (literal `**` leaked across all chapters). Added pure `splitBold` + `parseRichText` to `parseLatex.ts` (math-masked; `parseLatex` itself untouched for the Word export), inline bold in `KatexRenderer` (fixes `**` everywhere), and a new `RichText` block component (paragraphs + `- ` bullet lists) for the `definition` field. Converted the genuinely list-shaped definitions to bullets (Probability 6, Vectors 2, Statistics 1); narrative definitions stay prose. 3 new visualizations (dice-sum-grid, venn-two-events, probability-tree) bring the set to 9; `VisualizationSlug` union + `renderVisualization` dispatcher extended. 839 vitest green; notes:lint + notes:latex clean; prepush green throughout. Also fixed a stale Phase-1 `notes-index-helper` fixture that used "Probability" as a no-notes chapter (the pre-push gate runs typecheck/lint/build, not vitest, so it had slipped through).

- **2026-05-28/29 — Self-sufficient `/notes` template: built on Statistics, ported to Vectors, then abstracted + documented.** Turned the two existing notes chapters into a complete self-study path and froze it as a reusable template. Per-concept ramp is now worked example → **self-check** (fresh problem, full solution behind one reveal) → **Level 1 practice set** (short mastery reps) → featured PYQ → traps, with a per-subtopic **mastery checkpoint** (5 bank PYQs interleaved across concepts via `pickInterleavedCheckpoint`), a per-subtopic **summary** + collapsible chapter **revision sheet** (both auto-derived via `deriveSummary` from `concept.formula` + `traps` — zero manual sync), and **3 interactive SVG visualizations per chapter** (Statistics: regression-line-fit / variance-squares / histogram-bin-slider; Vectors: vector-addition / projection / cross-product-area). Statistics has 31 concept units (6 foundations + 25 bank, 29 with practice, ~116 reps); Vectors 28 (6 + 22, 27 with practice, 108 reps). Key decisions: **(1) Level 1 practice is AUTHORED per-concept, not bank-derived** — a difficulty audit (`question_concept_tags` × difficulty) showed per-concept EASY bank coverage is too thin (10 concepts have 0 easy PYQs); bank-derived leveling only works at subtopic granularity, and mastery reps want procedurally-near-identical problems PYQs don't give. **(2) The faded-example rung was built then REMOVED** — `FadedExampleCard` always rendered the answer and the "hidden" step's value reappeared in a later visible step, so the reveal gained nothing (a user screenshot proved it); it was also redundant with self-check. Don't re-add. **(3) Density fix: the practice half (self-check + reps) collapses behind one native `<details>` "Practice this concept" disclosure** per concept — stacking the full ramp inline made a ~40-screen scroll wall; teaching half (intuition → formula → worked → traps) stays visible. **(4) LaTeX hygiene: added `npm run notes:latex`** (`scripts/notes-latex-audit.ts`) — catches LaTeX leaking into the 5 plain-text-rendered fields (title, oneLineDefinition, whyItMatters, concept.name, formula.label) + unbalanced delimiters; fixed 2 real leaks and normalized in-math unicode (`°`→`^\circ`, `✓✗`→words, `÷`→`/`, `≠`→`\neq`). **(5) Template abstraction:** the two duplicated ~260-line page files became one `NotesSubtopicPage` + one `NotesChapterLanding` (shared server components driven by a `NotesChapterRegistration`; added `subjectDisplay` to the registry); the 4 route files are now ~15-line wrappers (`getNotesChapterBySlug(...)` + delegate). Adding a 3rd chapter = write `_data` + append one registry entry + clone the two thin wrappers + tag the bank. SSG routes byte-identical; 825/825 vitest, notes-lint + notes:latex clean. Full structure + workflow in the file-layout + "Notes editorial workflow" sections above.

- **2026-05-26 — NDA paper metadata canonicalized (4 SQL UPDATEs, 510 q).** NDA papers follow a strict convention — April = NDA 1, September = NDA 2; UPSC has never run a retest or "Shift 2". The bank had 4 anomaly batches where upload-time metadata was entered wrong: (1) 2025 Apr "Shift 2" / 120 q Maths → corrected to "NDA 1" (Maths section of NDA-1 Apr 2025; the matching 150 q GAT was already "NDA 1"); (2) 2018 Sep "Shift 2" / 120 q Maths → "NDA 2"; (3) 2023 Sep "NDA 1" / 120 q Maths → "NDA 2"; (4) 2017 Apr "NDA 2" / 150 q GAT → `pyq_month` changed to "Sep" (the missing Sep-2017 NDA-2 GAT section that completes that paper from 120 → 270 q). The first three were Maths-section misfiles (a full NDA paper = 150 GAT + 120 Maths = 270; the GAT half was tagged right but the Maths half got "Shift 2" or "NDA 1" instead of matching the GAT). The fourth was a whole-batch month error. User verified Pattern B against PDFs before commit. Post-fix state: every (year, month, note) tuple is canonical — 18 paper-batches × 270 q each, every Apr⇒NDA 1, every Sep⇒NDA 2, no Shift labels. 2020 Sep is genuinely absent (UPSC cancelled it for COVID); 2026 Sep hasn't happened yet. **No `upload_jobs` rows updated** — those reflect audit truth (what was uploaded at the time). **No content_hash impact** — hash is over text+options+answer, not metadata. Bank count unchanged at 4,860 NDA. **The canonicalization unlocked the bracket helper's simple `examName === "NDA"` rule** — without it the helper would have needed a per-(year,month) anomaly table. Captured as a [[nda-paper-canonical]] memory + a doc-the-rule sentence here for future upload audits: when an NDA upload arrives with `pyq_month != pyq_note`'s canonical, treat as a metadata bug and verify against source PDF before commit.
- **2026-05-26 — TEACHER edit link surfacing on /browse (commit a44016a).** Migration 0025 (same day, earlier) gave TEACHER edit access on the backend but the discoverability link on /browse stayed gated to `isAdmin`. Teachers signed in and saw no way to reach `/questions/[id]/edit`. Renamed the `isAdmin` prop on `QuestionList` + `QuestionCard` to `canEdit`; gate widened to `member?.role === "ADMIN" || member?.role === "TEACHER"`. The edit page itself still threads `isAdmin` separately to hide the Delete button + Visibility toggle (admin-only inside the form, server-rejected via `applyEdit`'s `forbidden_field` guard). `isAdmin` prop on QuestionCard had a single consumer (the Edit link conditional), so the rename had zero collateral. Per the [[click-to-reveal-admin-bypass]] rule, `isAdmin` is for chrome that genuinely needs admin gating; `canEdit` is the right primitive for "show the Edit link" since Edit is an action both roles can perform. No test changes (UI gate, no logic) per the [[tdd-calibration]] rule.
- **2026-05-26 — Dropped 10 cross-link banners from `/guide/nda-maths` (commit 8ae6fba).** The landing page carried 9 cross-subject "Also new: NDA [English/Physics/…] strategy guide" banners + 1 "/notes/nda-maths/statistics" banner — ~230 lines of JSX (lines 73–329) pushing the actual NDA Maths content (chapter table + principles + strategy) below the fold. User flagged "such references in guide are not necessary" + "look too cluttered". Removed all 10 + dropped 9 unused lucide icon imports (`Atom`, `FlaskConical`, `Globe`, `Landmark`, `Languages`, `Leaf`, `Newspaper`, `Scale`, `TrendingUp`); kept `NotebookPen` (still used in chapter table chip) + `ArrowRight` (used elsewhere). `nda-maths/page.tsx` collapsed 466 → 208 lines, restoring structural parity with the other 7 multi-route guide landings (181–184 lines). **Same pattern as the 2026-05-21 `/browse` Hero pill row removal** — both shipped when guides were new and discoverability needed scaffolding, both became pure duplication once PrimaryNav (Bank · Guides · Notes) + `/guide/nda` exam-picker + `/nda` exam home + Footer covered the same surface. Lesson worth recording as a feedback memory: [[stale-scaffolding-decay]] — "New:" / "Also new:" prefixes and cross-link banners decay once primary IA covers discoverability; revisit them every few months and prune. **Note**: this contradicts the 2026-05-18 `feedback_no_guide_meta_commentary.md` line "Cross-link banners to sibling guides are fine and useful — they're navigation, not meta-commentary"; the rule's been refined — cross-links are still fine as PRIMARY navigation (Footer, PrimaryNav, `/guide/nda` grid), but stale once a redundant landing-page banner reaches mass.
- **2026-05-26 — Word export underline fix: bypass OMML borderBox for `\underline{\text{...}}` and `\underline{\textit{...}}`.** Teacher reported the underlined keyword in NDA English antonym questions wasn't visible in the exported Word file (e.g. Q5 `01d50cab` "He nodded `absently` throughout the meeting." — rendered with no underline on `absently`). Root cause: the 2026-05-13 convention routed `\(\underline{\text{word}}\)` through `temml → mml2omml`, emitting `<m:borderBox>` with `hideTop/hideLeft/hideRight="on"`. The OMML markup is technically correct (only the bottom border drawn = supposed underline) and `tests/underline-roundtrip.test.ts` confirmed those markers landed in `word/document.xml` — but Word's actual rendering of an inline-math borderBox suppresses the bottom border under the `<m:mathPr defJc="left" wrapIndent="0" lMargin="0" rMargin="0">` defaults we inject for fraction alignment. The convention had been TDD-verified at the XML-markup level but never visually verified in Word. **Fix**: bypass the math pipeline for the documented underline patterns. New `OmmlSegment` variant `{ type: "underlined-text"; content; italic }` in [src/lib/export/ommlBuilder.ts](src/lib/export/ommlBuilder.ts) emitted when the whole inline-math segment matches `UNDERLINE_BYPASS_RE` (`^\s*\\underline\{\s*\\(text|textit)\{([^{}]+)\}\s*\}\s*([.,;:!?]?)\s*$`). `mathRuns` in [docxBuilder.ts](src/lib/export/docxBuilder.ts) renders the new variant as a native Word run — `TextRun({ text, underline: { type: "single" }, italics: italic ? true : undefined })` — producing `<w:r><w:rPr><w:u w:val="single"/></w:rPr><w:t>…</w:t></w:r>` directly, which renders rock-solid in every Word version, Word Online, LibreOffice, Google Docs. Trailing-punctuation capture handles the real-bank pattern where authors put sentence-ending `.,;:!?` INSIDE the math delimiters (`\(\underline{\text{insidious}}.\)`); the punctuation splits into a separate text segment so the rendered sentence keeps its full stop. Italic variant covers the taxonomy-formatting question (`860087ed`) where option D is `\(\underline{\textit{Amoeba}}\)` — without the underline, option D becomes visually identical to option A and the question is unsolvable. **What didn't change**: DB convention (`\(\underline{\text{word}}\)` stays in the bank — no backfill); browser KaTeX preview (KaTeX uses CSS `text-decoration: underline`); the `latexToOmml` borderBox path itself (preserved as fallback for direct callers — `/notes` preview + non-export surfaces — with isolated unit-test coverage so the LaTeX pipeline still has regression protection). **Audit + safety net**: new `scripts/audit-underline-patterns.ts` (`npm run audit:underlines`) scans every PUBLIC question's text/context/solution + every option for `\(\underline{...}\)` segments, classifies each as "covered" (bypassed → native underline) or "fall-through" (still routes through borderBox), prints a ranked list of distinct fall-through patterns. Live-bank run after the fix: **639/639 payloads covered, 0 fall-through**. The first run caught 5 trailing-period cases (`\(\underline{\text{insidious}}.\)`) that drove the regex extension. Fold into the post-upload ritual after each new English / Biology batch to catch silent-fall-through regressions. **Why "extend regex" over "fix the 5 DB rows"**: future uploads will keep producing trailing-punctuation-inside-delimiters patterns (extraction prompt doesn't enforce delimiter placement). Author-defensive in code beats chasing data each batch. Test churn: existing `underline-roundtrip.test.ts` updated — kept the LaTeX-pipeline borderBox assertion, inverted the docx-export assertion from "find `<m:borderBox>`" to "find `<w:u w:val="single"/>` AND don't find `<m:borderBox>` wrapping the same payload"; new fall-through test asserts non-matching shapes (`\(\underline{x}\)`) still route through OMML. New `omml-builder.test.ts` cases (10) cover the bypass + trailing-punctuation + each fall-through shape. Total: 776/776 vitest green, prepush clean. Out of scope (0 occurrences in bank): bold-underline (`\textbf` variant), mixed math+underline payloads, chained `\text{}\text{}`, block-math underline — all fall through to the still-broken OMML path with same behaviour as today (no new regression).
- **2026-05-26 — TEACHER role can edit questions + admin Members management UI (migration 0025, commit 683d9b2).** Reverses the long-standing "TEACHER is read-only" stance set during M2 (migration 0004). Migration `0025_teacher_editor_role.sql` adds `private.current_user_can_edit_questions()` helper (true for `org_members.role IN ('ADMIN','TEACHER')`); rewrites `questions UPDATE` policy → editor; splits the old `admin write options` FOR ALL into `admin insert options` + `editor update options` + `admin delete options`; opens the three `storage.objects` policies on the `question-images` bucket from admin-only → editor (TEACHER + ADMIN can upload/update/delete in own-org folder); adds `last_edited_by uuid references auth.users(id)` + `last_edited_at timestamptz` columns on `questions` + partial index. `src/lib/auth.ts` gains `requireEditor()` (401 if not signed in, 403 if role not in {ADMIN, TEACHER}). `applyEdit` signature gains optional `editorUserId` + `callerRole` params — stamps audit fields when given, AND returns new `kind: "forbidden_field"` result if `callerRole === "TEACHER"` AND `payload.visibility !== existing.visibility` (server-side guard against tampered requests; route maps to HTTP 403). `PUT /api/questions/[id]` + `POST /api/questions/[id]/leave-set` swap from `requireAdmin()` → `requireEditor()`. `/questions/[id]/edit` accepts TEACHER (was admin-only redirect to /dashboard); EditQuestionForm receives `isAdmin` prop and hides Delete button + Visibility section when `false`. `/dashboard` redirects TEACHER → /browse (admin-only territory). New `/dashboard/members` admin UI (page.tsx + MembersClient.tsx) wraps `/api/admin/members` POST endpoint (single action-discriminator route: `list | create | reset | remove | update_role`). Backed by new `src/lib/members/admin.ts` — pure validators + 5 DB helpers using `createSupabaseAdminClient()` (service-role; `auth.admin.*` needs it). Provisioning shape mirrors NDA Tracker's `TeachersTab.jsx` + `/api/teacher-account`: admin types **name + email + password + role** → auth user created with `user_metadata.name` stamped, `org_members` row inserted in one operation. Last-admin protection + self-protection (caller can't remove themselves OR demote themselves) enforced in the helpers. Test coverage: 32 new (8 validators + 5 RLS smoke + 5 audit+visibility-guard + `storage-rls.test.ts` updated for the opened editor write). All 764/764 vitest green; advisor lints unchanged (only the pre-existing `rate_limits no-policy` info + auth leaked-password-protection warning, both intentional). **Why:** user asked to enable teachers to edit questions, taking the NDA Tracker auth-shape as a reference. Borrowed: name + email + password admin form, serverless function with service-role, `user_metadata.role` stamping. Did NOT borrow: NDA Tracker's read-only-by-UI constraint (QB's TEACHER writes; the gating is in RLS + route guard, not just convention). Email serves as username (Supabase auth is email-native); the "non-email username" alternative was offered and the user picked email for simplicity.
- **2026-05-26 — Word export polish: student-first set banner + flush-left layout + lowercase option labels + per-question spacing (commits 92034c4, ac509df, 4d6b851).** Four coordinated render-time fixes to the `.docx` export, all in `src/lib/export/docxBuilder.ts` (+ new pure helper `src/lib/export/stripPassageCount.ts`). **(1) Student-first set banner:** when a user exports a subset of a set's siblings (cart picks just one, filter excludes others, extraction missed some), the verbatim PYQ banner "Set: …for the three (03) items that follow" promised items not on the page. Replaced with `Common context for questions X-Y:` where X-Y is the 1-indexed position of the first + last sibling in the export's question list (computed from a running position counter across the `groupBySet` loop), AND the passage is run through `stripPassageCountPhrase` which regex-strips the count claim (`for (the next |the )?\w+\s*(\(\d+\))?\s*items? that follows?` → `for the items that follow`). Bank text stays verbatim in DB; only render is normalised. Set-of-1 in the export falls through to inline `Context:` (no banner — a one-question "Set:" framing reads worst). **(2) Flush-left numbering:** changed `{left: 360, hanging: 360}` → `{left: 0, hanging: 0}` + `LevelSuffix.SPACE`. Wrap lines come back to column 0 instead of 0.25", aligning with options + Context + Set banner. **(3) Lowercase option labels:** `(A)/(B)/(C)/(D)` → `(a)/(b)/(c)/(d)` in both question paper AND answer key (`opt.label.toLowerCase()` at render; DB still stores uppercase). **(4) Per-question spacing:** one empty paragraph after every question (standalones AND set siblings) in the paper so the student's eye has a visible boundary between Q_n's option D and Q_{n+1}'s stem. The trailing blank after a set group moved to per-sibling instead, no double blank. Answer key: plain mode stays tight (one paragraph per question), solution mode gets the same blank between (answer + solution) blocks. New tests: `tests/docx-set-rendering.test.ts` (3 tests) + `tests/docx-spacing.test.ts` (5 tests) + `tests/strip-passage-count.test.ts` (10 unit tests). Existing `tests/docx-layout.test.ts` + `tests/docx-export.test.ts` updated for new numbering shape + lowercase labels. **Why:** progression across 3 commits — flush-left came from user feedback that wrap lines weren't aligned with the rest of the doc; lowercase was a same-day add; the K-of-N banner replacement was the third iteration after Option A (set-of-1 only) and Approach 2 (K-of-N from DB) — user pushed back that banner overpromise still happened on 2-of-3 cases, and the student-POV reframe ("student doesn't know what 'bank' means") drove Approach 4 which names the actual question numbers. Inter-question spacing was the fourth ask in the same review.


### 2026-05-27 — NDA Mathematics audit per-chapter detail (archived after consolidated summary)

These 26 per-chapter `/latex-cleanup` and `/solution-cleanup` entries from 2026-05-27 are archived here because the consolidated milestone entry in CLAUDE.md ("NDA · Mathematics CONTENT-CORRECTNESS AUDIT COMPLETE: 13-chapter batch closes the subject") summarizes the same batch, and the per-chapter at-a-glance register lives in `[[content-audit-progress]]` memory (32-row table with key flips + UPSC misprints per chapter). Kept here in newest-on-top order for deep-context lookups.

- **2026-05-27 — Solution cleanup: NDA · Mathematics · Differential Equations (chapter closure; follow-on to same-day /latex-cleanup Bucket B).** 2 wrong-key flips resolved autonomously: Q77 2020-Apr HARD `c05e4ee2` D(-4θdφ) → **B(-θdφ)** — differentiate \(e^{\theta\varphi}=c+4\theta\varphi\) using \(d(\theta\varphi)=\varphi d\theta+\theta d\varphi\), factor \((\varphi d\theta+\theta d\varphi)(e^{\theta\varphi}-4)=0\); the e^{θφ}≠4 branch gives \(\varphi d\theta=-\theta d\varphi\), with NO factor of 4 (key D's −4 coefficient was the error). Q99 2018-Sep HARD `7a0c2752` A(coefficient 4) → **C(coefficient 1)** — substitute \(v=x+y\), get \(dv/dy=2v/(v-1)\); separating and integrating gives \((v-\ln|v|)=2y+c\), so \(y-x+\ln(x+y)=c\) with coefficient 1 on the log (key A's "4 ln" was the error). 2 hash refreshes; both Q77 and Q99 self-flagged with REVIEW. **NDA Maths chapter closure: 18 of 31 fully closed** (adds Differential Equations + Quadratic Equations to Definite Integration / Sets & Relations / Complex Numbers / Application of Derivatives / P&C / Limits&Cont / Differentiation / S&S / Functions / Vectors / Lines / M&D / Statistics / Trig Identities / Probability / 3D Geometry).

Q8 2019-Apr `6a45f39e` solution rewrite (key D stays) — original solution had mid-derivation "Actually" / "Re-check" hedge pivots plus an arithmetic error ("x²-8=0" — should be x²=4 for the second case); clean rewrite produces roots \(\{-2,2,4\}\) via two cases (\(x\geq -2\) constraint from RHS). 1 hash refresh (Q1); Q8 solution-only edit (no hash impact). **Cluster note**: this is the 5th NDA Maths chapter today with a 2017–2021 paper-batch wrong-key (here 2021-Sep) reinforcing the LLM-extraction-prompt drift pattern.

- **2026-05-27 — Solution cleanup: NDA · Mathematics · Definite Integration (chapter closure; **stealth wrong-key caught by close-reading, not probe**).** 2 Bucket B deferrals resolved autonomously, zero PDFs needed. **2 key flips**: Q93 2026-Apr S18 `6f22751b` A(0) → **C(2)** — classic king's-property `∫_0^a f(x)/(f(x)+f(a-x))dx = a/2` with a=4 gives I=2. **Sibling Q94** `a5cc77c9` at correct key C=2 (its solution literally says "same as Q93") — independent confirmation that Q93 should be C, not A. Sibling unchanged. Q96 2022-Sep `0886bac8` D(0) → **A((e²−1)/e)** — **stealth wrong-key**: bank's solution claimed both terms of \(\int_{-\pi/2}^{\pi/2}(e^{\cos x}\sin x+e^{\sin x}\cos x)dx\) are odd, but second term is NOT odd (\(e^{\sin(-x)}\cos(-x)=e^{-\sin x}\cos x \neq -e^{\sin x}\cos x\) since exponentials are positive). Direct substitution \(u=\sin x\) gives \(\int_{-1}^{1}e^u du = e - 1/e = (e^2-1)/e\), matching option A. **Caught by close-reading the solution prose AFTER the unicode probe flagged the question for an unrelated `→` arrow** — neither `matches_option_disagrees_with_key` nor REVIEW-marker probes caught this since both solution and key agreed on the (wrong) value 0. **Probe-coverage gap pattern surfaced**: when bank solution + UPSC key both agree on a wrong answer with internally-consistent (but mathematically wrong) prose, current probes can't detect it; only close-reading of every flagged solution catches it. Worth a memory note for future audits — when a question's solution surfaces for ANY reason, sanity-check the math before assuming key is correct just because the probes agree. **2 hash refreshes**; single-correct-option invariant preserved; all Phase 5 probes return 0 across 66 q. **NDA Maths chapter closure: 16 of 31 fully closed** (adds Definite Integration to Sets & Relations / Complex Numbers / Application of Derivatives / P&C / Limits&Cont / Differentiation / S&S / Functions / Vectors / Lines / M&D / Statistics / Trig Identities / Probability / 3D Geometry).

- **2026-05-27 — Solution cleanup: NDA · Mathematics · Vectors (follow-on to same-day /latex-cleanup Bucket B).** All 11 Bucket B deferrals resolved in one DO block, **zero PDFs needed** — every flagged item was either a clean key flip (derivation lands EXACTLY on one of the four listed options, just not the keyed one) or a hedge-prose rewrite (derivation already matches the key). **9 key flips with math justification** verified before applying: Q70 2019 Apr `40bc2170` B(=2)→D(=4) (dot=0: 4λ=16); Q66 2018 Sep `cbd74c22` B(=6)→D(=5) (parallelogram law: \|a+b\|²=2(9+16)−25=25); Q67 2019 Apr `8b636e01` C(√43)→D(√53) (\|AB\|²=1+36+16); Q96 2022 Apr `22b8735b` D(2/3,8/3)→C(8/3,2/3) (collinearity p/4=q/1=2/3 gives p=8/3,q=2/3 — key had p,q labels swapped); Q65 2018 Sep `b60f495b` D(+k̂)→A(−k̂) (cross product k-component = (2)(−4)−(−1)(3)=−5); Q45 2017 Sep `2a0ba1f7` C(√3/2)→B(2√3) (δ=−2(1,−1,−1), \|δ\|=2√3); Q70 2025 Sep `20886e44` D(=2)→A(=−2) (moment x-component 20+2λ=16 ⇒ λ=−2); Q53 2026 Apr `1815d449` A(I only)→D(Neither) — set context complete in DB (P=(−2,−3,5)…), PQ∦RS by ratio test, PR·QS=4≠0; Q68 2026 Apr `ba5cc853` A(I only)→B(II only) — uses Q67's orthonormal setup, LHS of I = 2[abc] ≠ RHS = [abc]. **2 solution-prose rewrites** (no key change): Q67 2026 Apr `c577f20d` removed REVIEW: hedge, derivation correctly supports key C unchanged; Q95 2023 Sep `3da05cd8` removed "Per key B" hedge, derivation correctly supports key B unchanged. **9 hash refreshes** (one per key flip; the 2 solution-only rewrites don't change `correct_label` so no hash impact). All Phase 5 probes return 0 across the full chapter (97 q). **Workflow refinement formalized mid-session**: a wrong-key candidate whose derivation lands EXACTLY on one of the four listed options is NOT corrupted — the bank is internally consistent, just mislabeled, and the fix is a direct key flip with math justification (no PDF needed). PDFs become load-bearing only when the derivation lands OUTSIDE the option set (printed-paper misprint candidate like Probability Q117 Apr 2023), the stem is truncated and DB context can't reconstruct setup, or constraints are logically inconsistent. Rule mirrored in both `.claude/commands/latex-cleanup.md` (Bucket B section + Phase 6 output template) and `.claude/commands/solution-cleanup.md` (Phase 3 screenshot policy). **Cross-chapter pattern**: Vectors had the highest wrong-key rate of any audited chapter (9/97 = 9.3%) — confirms that /notes editorial passes clean stems but never audit keys, so wrong-keys accumulate disproportionately in /notes-touched chapters. Cluster sub-pattern: 2 candidates each in 2026 Apr · 2019 Apr · 2018 Sep (broader bias than Functions's single-paper 2017 Sep cluster). Cross-chapter wrong-key queue from prior chapters: 17 candidates remain (M&D 5 + Statistics 5 + Trig Identities 6 + Probability 1 PDF-pending) — Vectors queue closed.
